This additive review supersedes only their procedural review status after its gates complete. It does not retroactively claim that the original source recovery was independently reviewed, and does not silently replace any scientific text.

# Identity-ledger source check: history labels do not yet identify a receiver

Date: 2026-11-03 America/Phoenix. Author: GPT Work.
Research parent: `1a18308a026c06882b428fbe201b4634c34dfd8f`.
Thread inspected: `bf40df6ae61e890e4ca4580979a43d8da812e78a`, T-000 and the recent exchange, plus comments through T-297 (`6606779324`). No subsequent reply or active Actions run was found at the start of this pass.

**Status: bounded historical-source recovery and type check. Native delivery NOT_DERIVED.** This is not a new theorem, independent review, exhaustive absence claim, or scientific execution. The thermal route remains closed.

## Why this is a concrete lead

The original v7.2/v7.3 history-algebra reports contain an object defined before any joint dilation: the identity/unit ledger. It has one projection for each history object and a canonical coefficient-selection operation. That is a more specific candidate for inherited ownership than another chosen coupling. The four reports preserved here were read completely. Their historical theorem and audit claims are not newly certified.

This pass did not repeat T-264/T-265's producer-name searches. Those entries explicitly limited their scope to named Python/Markdown patterns in the repository. The inspected shared thread has no occurrence of “groupoid” or “identity ledger”; that limited check does not establish that these sources have never been considered elsewhere.

## Admission questions and strongest positive reading

1. **Missing relation:** does an original identity-labelled algebra make a surviving identity the owner of an accessible output, rather than just its mathematical label?
2. **Concrete operations:** v7.2's object functional `omega_u`; v7.3's projection `E_0` onto the unit algebra; v7.23's quotient observable map `Phi_C`.
3. **Adversary:** a joint realization that has the required source update while the fixed designated R is blind. The W_R/W_E comparison is applicable only after a typed link to that benchmark is supplied or earned.
4. **Desired consequence:** an effect belonging to the same R before and after the event, initially blind and necessarily separating the preparations afterward.
5. **Falsifier at source inspection:** the operations only evaluate history identities or map source observables, with receiver association absent; or their prerequisite sector is only conditional.

The favorable fact is real within the declared algebraic setting: object identities provide distinguished projections without choosing a detector after a realization. The question is whether their meaning includes receiver ownership. It does not follow merely from their being distinguished.

## What the actual maps do

| Source and section | Domain and operation | Output actually identified | Receiver-delivery boundary |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| [v7.2](sources/v7_2.md), Theorem and Boundary | Given a genuinely reversible history sector, complex-linearize its groupoid; `omega_u(a)` extracts the coefficient of `1_u` | A positive object-indexed functional on the history algebra | A history-object label is not identified with an independently retained receiver subsystem. Irreversible pruning remains outside the groupoid premise. |
| [v7.3](sources/v7_3.md), T1 | `E_0 : C[G] -> span{1_u}`, retaining only identity coefficients | The entire identity ledger within the same algebra | It is a projection to existing identity observables; it is not a source-to-receiver record-writing map. |
| v7.3, T2 | Retain coefficients in a wide, dagger-closed, composition-closed subgroupoid | A retained subgroupoid algebra | The report explicitly requires actual pruning to be proved to have this form. “Wide” retains all objects; this is not automatically the P1 removal/retraction of address objects. |
| [v7.16](sources/v7_16.md), Correction and Why this does not yet close the native bridge | Chart changes `T_ij=B_j^{-1}B_i` and their inverses | A pair groupoid on atlas charts | The report distinguishes this matrix atlas from full native generated-algebra transport, whose preservation of source/provenance and retained-order structure remains open there. Chart identities must not silently become P1 receiver identities. |
| [v7.23](sources/v7_23.md), Main result | Require a UCP map `Phi_C : A_Y -> A_X`, classical ledger compatibility and within-fiber permutation invariance | Coarse-source observables pulled back to the fine-source algebra | The compatibility equation is a premise of the classification. No output algebra for a separate R is named. |

Premise tags: the named formulas and qualifications are **INHERITED historical mathematical definitions**, conditional on their explicitly stated sectors. Their use as the actual event, a correspondence from history/chart objects to P1 addresses, and identification of the output with R are **SUPPLIED** unless separately derived. Actual event occurrence remains **ACCEPTED INPUT**. This inspection does not reopen occurrence or demand a quantum representation before a native classical consequence could count.

## Two direct checks against a false ownership inference

**Identity selection is not acquisition.** For the unit `e_a=1_a`, v7.3's literal formula gives `E_0(e_a)=e_a`. If one models the state update by the dual of this particular map, then for every input state `rho`, `(rho o E_0)(e_a)=rho(e_a)`. Thus a pair initially equal on the unit ledger remains equal on that ledger under this operation. This is immediate substitution in the recovered definition, not a new pruning no-go. It does not say what a different sourced operation could do. In particular, `E_0` deletes nonidentity history coefficients; it does not sum a removed object's coefficient into its nearest retained ancestor.

**Quotient compatibility is still a source equation.** v7.23 requires

\[
\Phi_C(E_{yy})=P_y=\sum_{x\in\pi^{-1}(y)}E_{xx}.
\]

Consequently its dual state map obeys

\[
\Lambda_C(\rho)(E_{yy})=\rho(P_y).
\]

This is the familiar retained-source aggregation on diagonal inputs. It is a valid fixed coarse observable within this declared model. It does not say `E_yy` is an accessible effect of R. Choosing the quotient to be the P1 retraction, even if justified separately, would not by itself provide that last identification. The report's unresolved coherence kernel concerns selection within a source-channel family; solving it would still require a separate argument about receiver ownership.

These checks keep three labels distinct: the groupoid object's `u`, the coarse quotient label `y`, and the actual retained receiver `r(u)`. Reusing a letter does not construct maps between those types.

## Standing W_R/W_E test

No typed native joint constraint K has emerged here. The recovered operations have no complementary E/R codomain, so there is no justified new W_R-pass/W_E-fail table. In particular, do not assume that the benchmark channels satisfy all of v7.23's fiber-symmetry hypotheses: no such correspondence was checked or is needed for this disposition. Nor does an informative removed-atom flag establish full owner-coordinate identity (T-296).

A condition imposed solely on a reduced source map cannot choose between realizations of that same map, but this general observation does not substitute for typing a particular historical model into the benchmark. The source discovery supplies a concrete formal identity object; it does not supply the missing native restriction on the joint event.

## Disposition and exact re-entry condition

The identity ledger is worth preserving as an original native-structure lead. Its own published operations do not remove a receiver-delivery premise. No larger groupoid enumeration, new kernel selector, GNS construction, or decoder is justified for the present target.

Re-entry requires an existing operation that identifies a history/retained identity with this receiver's accessible algebra **and transports that association through the event**, or forces one effect in that algebra without full state identification. Initial identification alone has already been tested in the owner-correspondence work; it is not new evidence for persistence.

No unique minimal physical postulate, archive-wide impossibility, or proto-gravity law follows. This packet records where the literal source maps stop, so future work need not rediscover the identity-ledger route by name.

## Preservation and verification limits

[SOURCE_IDENTITIES.json](SOURCE_IDENTITIES.json) records source IDs, original titles/dates/reported lengths, complete-read status, and hashes for the transcriptions. Each is complete extracted text plus one final LF. Matching reported lengths is not proof of original-byte identity. Original ZIPs, historical scripts, numerical results and historical certification were not replayed.

Only transcription integrity, scope, and immutable publication are checked in this pass. No scientific module, new Actions workflow or Gemini call is warranted for this source disposition. No existing proof, acceptance, branch or thermal archive is edited.

New sources: complete extracted-text transcriptions of v9.179 and v9.206. Historical certification labels, equations and recorded numerical results are preserved as source content, not newly endorsed.

## Question

Do native graph incidence or source provenance already contain the operation that binds an actual event to a designated retained receiver's output?

T-318 recovered fixed mathematical subsystem factors and graph incidences. This continuation follows two later reports that expressly use those primitives. It does not repeat the P1 infrastructure or introduce a new information channel.

## Positive content retained

Native incidence already singles out mathematical factor pairs before a candidate interaction is specified. The v9.179 construction then gives a concrete, source-reported pair effect under its explicitly hypothesized operation. This is stronger than having no named subsystem or no possible informative mechanism.

But the generating assignment in §1 is declared as H179, not derived from incidence or compatibility. The source calls it a reversible candidate interaction relation, not pruning. Its parameter theta is a dimensionless probe parameter, not physical time.

## Ownership and comparison limits

1. The v9.179 preparation difference is a hidden **joint correlation** between specimens, with the same one-specimen marginals. It is not automatically the current P1 single-source difference.
2. A joint pair may qualify as a receiver if its ownership and available observations are independently earned. No such identification with the current P1 owner r(u) is established here. A pair contrast cannot be assigned to either constituent's marginal when those marginals are equal.
3. No mapping of the six five-level factors to the benchmark source, E and R is sourced. Consequently this inspection assigns **no W_R/W_E pass/fail score**.
4. The v9.206 result concerns preparation selection from one-copy data. Do not promote it to a no-go for all native joint-event updates, or infer that one extra scalar is sufficient to establish receiver delivery.
5. Fixed graph incidence does not prohibit state change. The missing item on this route is a derived operation and its receiver correspondence, not necessarily new incidence or a changed admissibility domain.
6. Actual atemporal measurement/pruning occurrence remains ACCEPTED INPUT. This source trace adds no demand to derive occurrence.

## Disposition and handoff

This is source clarification, not a new native interaction result. Do not rerun H179, optimize a generator/readout, continue the suggested v9.180 observability campaign, introduce a copy-provenance law, or reopen the thermal/E-alpha branch.

An admissible next lead must name an existing operation that binds an occurring event to a receiver-owned retained output, or one receiver-owned effect. Named factors, support connectivity, a supplied joint preparation, and a successful conditional pair readout do not by themselves supply that binding.

T-319-dot has already acknowledged the b-source / r-receiver correction to T-315. There is no outstanding demand from that reply and no need for another routine acknowledgement.

# UQCF–GEM v9.179 — Connected Native-Incidence Interaction Gate

## Adjudication

**CONNECTED_PAIR_DENSITY_IMPRINT_ACTIVATES_HIDDEN_PAIR_MODE__COUPLING_POSTULATED**

The gate constructs a new, explicitly hypothesized interaction from the already
specified compatible pair density and the marked native A–B incidences. In the
same-source two-specimen example, this interaction converts a selected hidden
completion correlation into an original-pair measurement difference. All terms
are two-body on the six original five-level factors. No extra coherent qutrit
reference, selected outcome, or fitted response coefficient is introduced.

The activation is proved by an exact invariant-subspace reduction, a nonzero
fourth-order response coefficient, and a rigorous small-angle remainder bound.
Original-space matrix calculations corroborate the result and quantify a much
smaller signal than the background state change.

This is a conditional operational mechanism, not a native source law or a gravity
breakthrough. The density-to-interaction assignment, specimen preparation relation,
copy registration, signs, strengths and unitary family remain physical hypotheses.
The pair density is a fixed operator-valued input. It is not updated from the
instantaneous evolving state, and no nonlinear feedback law is derived.

## Version and evidence

v9.178 explicitly proposed this gate as v9.179. v9.172 remains reserved for the
separate saturation-boundary audit; B1 remains an unnumbered precursor. The
supplied v9.178 report was read in full. Its unchanged v9.173 parent constructor
is retained in reference/ and rerun for both arrangements.

The local gate specification follows analytic planning, including exploratory
8-dimensional calculations, and precedes the recorded final checker. It is NOT
external preregistration. The main hypothesis uses all marked native A–B edges
and the complete supplied compatible pair density, not a subset selected by
maximizing the hidden signal. Ablations diagnose the resulting mechanism.

## 1. New H179: fixed pair-density imprint on native incidences

One specimen is S=A B1 B2, with each factor five-dimensional. A registered second
specimen is S'=A' B1' B2'. Native edges in each specimen are A–B1 and A–B2. Let
sigma be the common, compatible pair marginal of the retained parent optimum T0.
This sigma is NOT the original incompatible target rho and NOT the optimal dual
witness W. The two arrangements have their own sigma, fixed before the probe.

Let F_x exchange the corresponding registered factor x,x', identity elsewhere.
Postulate the Hermitian generator

\[
 K=g_AF_A+g_1F_{B_1}+g_2F_{B_2}
   +\lambda_1(\sigma_{AB_1}+\sigma_{A'B'_1})
   +\lambda_2(\sigma_{AB_2}+\sigma_{A'B'_2}).                 \tag{1}
\]

Every embedded sigma acts on its two original factors only. Added spectators
remain outside the marked supports. Equation (1) can equivalently be specified
by its expectation on all joint densities: add the marked exchange expectations
and the overlaps of each native pair marginal with the supplied sigma. Such an
expectation clause fixes the operator by linearity; it does not establish the
physical truth or uniqueness of the clause.

Motivation: test whether interaction at the existing relational incidences can
connect the three otherwise independent role channels of v9.178. The whole
local pair description is used without an eigenvector or hidden-coherence
selection. Interpreting a density operator as an interaction coefficient is a
NEW constitutive hypothesis, not a theorem of compatibility. It is not a choice
of the optimizer as a state evolution, and it is not a claim that an unknown
state can program this gate from one copy without resources.

The primary convention is g_A=g_1=g_2=lambda_1=lambda_2=1. Partner exchange
requires equal B coefficients, not equality with g_A or with lambda. Strength,
relative strength and sign are not derived. Set U(theta)=exp(-i theta K).
Theta is a dimensionless probe angle, not emergent or external physical time.
The law is a reversible candidate interaction relation, not pruning.

## 2. Valid inputs, spectators and covariance

Retain t=21/41,z=16 and

\[
 e=|0,4,4\rangle,\quad f=|2,1,1\rangle,\quad Z=|e\rangle\langle f|,
 \quad b=9/820,\quad h=9/1640.
\]

T0 has isolated diagonal entries b on e,f. The same-source joint input is

\[
 \Omega(m)=T_0\otimes T_0+m(Z\otimes Z^\dagger+Z^\dagger\otimes Z),
 \qquad m=\kappa h^2,\quad 0\le\kappa\le1.                 \tag{2}
\]

The modified block on |e,f>,|f,e> has diagonal b^2 and off-diagonal m;
its eigenvalues are b^2 +/- m, strictly positive at the selected m=h^2.
Normalization and all one-specimen marginals are unchanged. All initial original
pair marginals coincide for every m. The complete initial specimen spectra
need not coincide; m is genuine hidden joint correlation, not a coordinate label.
The preparation is supplied, as in v9.175–176, not produced by cloning or a source
operation. H176 need not be true for this two-specimen calculation: any retained
preparation with the same kappa has the same Omega_2.

For any spectator E and any correlated state Xi on the active system plus E,

\[
 \operatorname{Tr}_E[(U\otimes I_E)\Xi(U^\dagger\otimes I_E)]
   =U\operatorname{Tr}_E(\Xi)U^\dagger.                     \tag{3}
\]

Hence spectators with unchanged active marginal are exactly neutral in every
dimension. Absorbing a spectator into an active role and redefining sigma or
F_x is a different interaction specification, not the same neutrality comparison.

Under local coordinate changes, transform sigma and the role registrations
along with all states and effects; K transforms by conjugation and probabilities
are unchanged. In the displayed parent coordinates each sigma commutes with
D tensor conjugate(D), so K also commutes with the parent common phase torus
on the six factors. No Hilbert-amplitude map is extracted from edge provenance.
With equal edge coefficients, K and the input are symmetric under partner
exchange, ensuring equal AB1 and AB2 outputs. Outputs need not stay optimal for
the original static compatibility problem.

## 3. An exact 8-dimensional invariant support inside dimension 15,625

Write u=(0,4,4), v=(2,1,1). For a placement bit string x=(x_A,x_1,x_2), put u_x
in the first specimen and v_x in the second when x=0 on that role, and reverse
them when x=1. The eight resulting coordinate kets span H_*.

These are positions of original labels across specimens, NOT new physical
qubits, pooled states, spatial positions or the 315/311 completion dimensions.
The full input difference in (2) lies on H_* and has normalized form

\[
 C=|000\rangle\langle111|+|111\rangle\langle000|,
 \qquad\Omega(m)-\Omega(0)=m C.                            \tag{4}
\]

Vertical role swaps preserve H_* and act as placement Pauli X_x.
For every ket in H_*, the A labels belong to {0,2}, while B labels belong to
{4,1}. They are disjoint. The parent sigma has only diagonal entries on these
A-not-equal-B coordinates; its non-diagonal entries connect only |ii> and |jj>.
Consequently all native pair-density terms preserve H_* too.

Define four actual pair populations

\[
 s_{04}=\langle04|\sigma|04\rangle,\quad
 s_{01},\ s_{24},\ s_{21},\qquad
 J_0=\tfrac12(s_{04}+s_{21}-s_{01}-s_{24}).                 \tag{5}
\]

For the parent class s_ab=(1-t)V_ab/50 when a!=b. Then, up to an irrelevant
identity term, the exact restriction of (1) is

\[
 \boxed{K_*=g_AX_A+g_1X_1+g_2X_2+J_1Z_AZ_1+J_2Z_AZ_2,\quad
 J_j=\lambda_j J_0.}                                     \tag{6}
\]

This is a three-placement-spin transverse-field Ising matrix. That label names
a standard matrix form; no Ising source or emergent spatial geometry is inferred.
For the two retained pair states,

\[
 J_0^A=1/41,\qquad J_0^B=4/205.                            \tag{7}
\]

Their scalar identity offsets at the primary strengths are 22/205 and 24/205.
The differences between arrangements are explained by already different LOCAL
pair populations. They are not attributed to different hidden-fiber dimensions.

The checker verifies all eight full-space support columns with exact radicals,
not just a projected Hamiltonian assumed to be closed. It separately constructs
the full 15,625-dimensional sparse generator and checks closure numerically.

## 4. Exact hidden-to-pair activation

Use the block population contrasts on original factors

\[
 D_A=|0\rangle\langle0|-|2\rangle\langle2|,\quad
 D_B=|4\rangle\langle4|-|1\rangle\langle1|,
 \quad E_{AB_1}=(I+D_A\otimes D_B)/2.                     \tag{8}
\]

E is a valid effect and invariant under the parent phase group. It is a stated
readout of the known hidden block; it was not used to choose K's native support
or its full pair-density coefficients. We also use the input effect sigma itself
and the complete pair trace norm. The original Bell projector is a negative
readout control.

For real m, the hidden pair difference is diagonal on (04,01,24,21). The
placement complement X_A X_1 X_2 commutes with K_* and C, so complementary
placement probabilities coincide. The trace of C is zero. These facts force

\[
 \Delta\sigma_{AB_1}(\theta)=\frac{mR_1(\theta)}2 D_A\otimes D_B,
 \quad
 R_1(\theta)=\frac12\operatorname{Tr}
 [Z_AZ_1e^{-i\theta K_*}Ce^{i\theta K_*}].                \tag{9}
\]

The second pair has the analogous R2. Hence

\[
 \Delta p_{AB_1}=mR_1,\qquad
 D(\sigma_m(\theta),\sigma_0(\theta))=|mR_1|.              \tag{10}
\]

All original single-factor hidden differences vanish for this real m,
but a pair correlation can now be nonzero. This is a change from v9.178.
The pair effect sigma already supplied as input has gap

\[
 \operatorname{Tr}(\sigma\Delta\sigma_{AB_1})=mJ_0R_1.     \tag{11}
\]

Thus the existence of the effect is not confined to a custom block diagnostic.
The Bell projector is insensitive to the same difference because it is supported
on |ii>, disjoint from (04,01,24,21).

### First nonzero coefficient and a rigorous finite interval

Repeated commutation in the exact eight-state matrix gives

\[
 \boxed{R_1(\theta)=-\tfrac83 J_2g_Ag_1g_2\theta^4+O(\theta^6),}
\]
\[
 \boxed{R_2(\theta)=-\tfrac83 J_1g_Ag_1g_2\theta^4+O(\theta^6).} \tag{12}
\]

All coefficients of orders zero through three vanish. Odd orders vanish for
these real symmetric input, generator and readout matrices. The exact sixth
coefficient for R1 is

\[
 \frac4{45}J_2g_Ag_1g_2
 (11J_1^2+6J_2^2+20g_1^2+6g_2^2+6g_A^2).
\]

The trace formula (9) is exact at every angle; (12) is explicitly a local
expansion, not a fitted full-angle formula. For the primary positive unit
strengths, ||K_*||<=3+2J0<4. The trace norm of C is 2 and ||E||<=1, so Taylor's
remainder after order five is bounded by

\[
 |R_1+(8/3)J_0\theta^4|\le\frac{2\,8^6}{6!}|\theta|^6.    \tag{13}
\]

For BOTH parent arrangements and 0<|theta|<=1/200, the right side is less than
(4/3)J0 theta^4. Therefore

\[
 \boxed{\Delta p_{AB_1}\le-(4/3)mJ_0\theta^4<0\quad(m>0).} \tag{14}
\]

This conservative interval proves actual nonzero activation, rather than only a
formal Taylor coefficient. The population sign is tied to declared readout and
coupling signs. It is not gravitational attraction.

### Balanced all-angle spectral reduction

When g_A=g_1=g_2=g and J1=J2=J, partner symmetry and placement complement reduce
the part accessed by C to two three-dimensional blocks:

\[
 K_+=\begin{pmatrix}2J&g&\sqrt2g\\g&-2J&\sqrt2g\\
 \sqrt2g&\sqrt2g&g\end{pmatrix},\quad
 K_-=\begin{pmatrix}2J&-g&\sqrt2g\\-g&-2J&\sqrt2g\\
 \sqrt2g&\sqrt2g&-g\end{pmatrix}.                          \tag{15}
\]

With Q=diag(1,-1,0) and v=(1,0,0), the exact function in (9) is one half of
<v|exp(i theta K+) Q exp(-i theta K+)|v> minus the analogous K- expression.
There is no numerical diagonalization threshold defining these sectors.

## 5. Mechanism controls: graph connectivity is necessary, not sufficient

1. **No native horizontal terms:** J1=J2=0 gives the v9.178 null exactly.
2. **Only A–B1 coupling:** J2=0 leaves AB1 blind to this hidden mode, at every
   angle. The B2 placement channel is still disconnected from that readout.
   Interchanging the indices gives the corresponding AB2 null.
3. **Bell-only connected graph:** replacing each horizontal source-density term
   by the parent Bell projector connects the same graph but annihilates every ket
   in H_*. The hidden trajectory is exactly the role-only trajectory. Bare graph
   connectivity alone does not imply operator-level access.
4. **Diagonal off-diagonal-coordinate part of sigma:** on H_* it has exactly the
   same action as full sigma. The hidden response is caused by the local
   population contrast (5) acting jointly with the exchanges, NOT by the Bell
   coherence part of sigma.
5. **Reverse the native-edge coefficient:** the leading sign reverses. The finite
   deterministic control uses lambda1=lambda2=-1 without any optimization.

For control 2, the omitted placement bit is dynamically separate. Tracing that
role removes its traceless tensor factor; equivalently, summing its diagonal
contribution in the placement reduction gives zero. The proof does not rely on
small-angle truncation.

Connected matrices and noncommuting couplings permit the access, but they are
standard quantum observability ingredients. The calculation supplies no claim
that this is a gravity-specific residue.

## 6. Numerically evaluated predictions at the fixed displayed point

At kappa=1, h=9/1640, unit strengths and theta=pi/4:

| Quantity | Arrangement A | Arrangement B |
|---|---:|---:|
| J0 | 1/41 | 4/205 |
| Pair probability difference | -3.6718640001e-7 | -2.9377271178e-7 |
| Pair trace distance | 3.6718640001e-7 | 2.9377271178e-7 |
| Source-effect sigma probability difference | -8.9557658538e-9 | -5.7321504738e-9 |

The larger pair differences are approximately -0.00003671864 and
-0.00002937727 **percentage points**, respectively. They are small. These decimal
values are evaluations of (9), corroborated in the full original space, not
claimed closed algebraic values at pi/4.

The original-space background and changed-input pure ancillary amplitudes give,
for the block diagnostic in arrangement A, approximately

- baseline probability: 0.5129317538309607;
- with hidden correlation: 0.5129313866445607.

The background state can change much more than this difference. Only the
subtracted, matched-input difference is the hidden activation signal. There are
no experimental observations or feasibility estimates for measuring it here.

## 7. What is and is not a new physical result

Relative to v9.178, the candidate now combines exact spectator neutrality,
connected original two-body support, common-phase covariance, and nonzero
same-source hidden-to-pair activation. No extra phase-sensitive detector or
reference qutrit was inserted. The second specimen's specified joint preparation
already carries the invariant relational resource.

The successful interaction does not follow merely from knowing that the original
pair states coexist. H179 declares that these pair matrices enter a Hamiltonian
linearly and are held fixed during the probe. Its strengths and their signs are
not selected by compatibility. This does NOT derive same-provenance identification,
relative registration, stress-energy, source magnitude, a universal force sign,
spatial dependence, gauge dynamics, spacetime, physical time, or pruning.

The exact small-angle response is fourth order for this selected input and
readout. It is not a Newtonian linear response or an inverse-square law.
The A/B response differences depend on local pair populations; do not attribute
them to the 315/311 extension-family dimension difference. No claim is made that
all hidden directions respond or that this mode is physically privileged.

The all-state fixed-image no-go is not contradicted: each unitary moves a state
inside the original finite-dimensional density-state domain. The admissibility
map is unchanged. Pair equality persists for the displayed exchange-symmetric
inputs but no preservation theorem for every nonsymmetric equal-marginal input
is asserted.

## 8. Computation and reproducibility

The final checker performs:

- the unchanged exact parent density/PSD/dual/equal-marginal checks for A and B;
- sixteen exact original-space invariant-support columns (eight per arrangement);
- symbolic nested commutators through order six for independent gA,g1,g2,J1,J2;
- exact balanced 3+3-sector matrices and the rational Taylor remainder bound;
- full entrywise integer-character covariance and specimen-exchange identities;
- 170 deterministic full-15,625-dimensional sparse exponential comparisons:
  two arrangements, five declared variants, seventeen angles;
- whole original-pair reductions from the propagated two-column hidden support,
  rather than only checking a scalar readout;
- full 15,625-by-625 positive background ancillary amplitudes propagated once per
  arrangement, then exact two-column block replacement to produce the propagated
  positive hidden-input amplitude and directly recompute its pair probability;
- a separate 64-dimensional complete-apparatus basis covariance check and a
  128-dimensional correlated-spectator test. These small tests are explicitly
  not presented as full five-level spectator simulations. The analytic proofs
  establish the general covariance and spectator statements.

The 8-dimensional model is an exactly embedded invariant support for the
**difference**. It is not a replacement for the full background state or a claim
that the whole quantum system has eight dimensions. The entire state is mixed on
the observed factors but represented by explicit pure ancillary amplitudes; no
physical mixing, discard or collapse is simulated.

The first checker invocation encountered an unevaluated symbolic zero in a strict
Python equality assertion. Simplifying that algebraic expression repaired the
implementation; the hypothesis, coefficients, cases and tolerances were unchanged.
The final logged run includes the added direct hidden-amplitude check. Results
are deterministic corroboration; no p-value, numerical universality, or external
preregistration is claimed.

# UQCF–GEM v9.206 — Copy-Provenance Primitive Gate

## Adjudication

\[
\boxed{
\texttt{FROZEN\_ONE\_COPY\_PROVENANCE\_CANNOT\_SELECT\_COPY\_PREPARATION}
}
\]

\[
\boxed{
\texttt{A\_SECOND\_ORDER\_COPY\_PROVENANCE\_DATUM\_IS\_MINIMAL\_FOR\_THE\_RESTRICTED\_FAMILY}
}
\]

The v9.205 stop criterion is met.

Within the inspected frozen UQCF–GEM architecture, native graph incidence and subsystem
factorization exist, but no native variable specifies how repeated source instances are jointly
prepared. Explicit replica/copy-role structures found in earlier gates were introduced as
candidate axioms, not extracted from the frozen one-copy provenance.

For the one-parameter exchangeable family \(R_n^{(\eta)}\) constructed in v9.205, every
one-copy density and every one-copy observable is independent of \(\eta\). A single
exchange-invariant two-copy statistic, however, recovers \(\eta\) exactly.

Therefore no selector factoring through the frozen one-copy data can choose the physical
multi-copy resource.

In this restricted family, one real second-order datum is both necessary and sufficient.
In the broader preparation-measure family of v9.175, no fixed finite specimen order determines
the full hierarchy, so this one-scalar conclusion is not promoted to a general theorem about
all copy provenance.

No gravity, force, gauge law, spacetime, pruning, or physical time is derived.

## 0. Ledger and retained boundaries

Retained Bridge / UQCF–GEM Adjudication Ledger R1–R3 remains binding.

- R1: cite the ledger before execution.
- R2: monotone concession.
- R3: state the diff against prior adjudications.

The frozen source-law boundary, the v9.72 native-incidence boundary, the v9.175
preparation-composition nonuniqueness theorem, the v9.193 one-source processor boundary,
and v9.204–205 connected-response resource results remain intact.

**Diff:** v9.205 proved that exchangeability and all-count projective consistency do not select
the two-copy resource. v9.206 asks whether frozen provenance contains any variable capable of
distinguishing that family. In the inspected architecture, it does not.

## 1. What frozen provenance actually supplies

The inspected native architecture supplies:

- fixed tensor factors;
- fixed graph/subsystem incidence;
- ordinary subsystem restriction;
- relabeling/covariance structure;
- canonical product construction for disjoint systems.

The earlier native-incidence audit explicitly separated this from:

- assigning extra independent factors to a source token;
- identifying copy roles;
- imposing equal joint obligations;
- declaring a physical preparation relation between repeated source instances.

Those additional structures appeared only in explicit candidate constructions.

A canonical product state is therefore **available** for disjoint inputs. That does not imply
that every pair of repeated instances of one physical source must be iid. Elevating the product
construction to that status is a strict independence/preparation axiom.

## 2. Forgetful-map theorem

Let \(\mathfrak E(\bar\rho)\) denote exchangeable, projectively consistent preparation
hierarchies whose one-copy marginal is \(\bar\rho\).

There is a natural restriction map

\[
\Pi_1:\mathfrak E(\bar\rho)\to\mathcal D(\mathcal H_S),
\qquad
\Pi_1(\{R_n\})=R_1.
\]

For v9.205,

\[
R_n^{(\eta)}
=
(1-\eta)\bar\rho^{\otimes n}
+
\eta
\left[
\frac12\rho_0^{\otimes n}
+
\frac12\rho_1^{\otimes n}
\right],
\qquad
0\le\eta\le1,
\]

and

\[
\Pi_1(R^{(\eta)})=\bar\rho
\]

for every \(\eta\).

Therefore every deterministic provenance quantity of the form

\[
P=f(\Pi_1(R))
\]

is constant on this entire family.

This includes every one-copy expectation value, one-copy spectrum, one-copy marginal,
one-copy completion property, and any source label or token count held fixed across the
comparison.

Thus:

\[
\boxed{
\text{no function of the frozen one-copy data can select }\eta.
}
\]

This is a simple factorization theorem, not an empirical claim.

## 3. A canonical second-order witness

Let \(S_{12}\) be the complete-copy SWAP between the two source specimens.

For any one-copy density \(\rho\),

\[
\operatorname{Tr}[(\rho\otimes\rho)S_{12}]
=
\operatorname{Tr}\rho^2.
\]

For the retained source

\[
\bar\rho=
\frac12\rho_0+\frac12\rho_1,
\qquad
\operatorname{Tr}\bar\rho^2=\frac12.
\]

For the v9.205 family,

\[
\boxed{
\langle S_{12}\rangle_\eta
=
\frac12+\frac{\eta}{2}.
}
\]

Hence

\[
\boxed{
\eta
=
\frac{\langle S_{12}\rangle-\operatorname{Tr}\bar\rho^2}
{1-\operatorname{Tr}\bar\rho^2}
=
2\langle S_{12}\rangle-1.
}
\]

The checker verifies this over 21 values of \(\eta\), with maximum recovery error
\(1.67\times10^{-16}\).

The two-copy trace distance from the iid point is also exact:

\[
\boxed{
D(R_2^{(\eta)},R_2^{(0)})=\frac{\eta}{2}.
}
\]

Thus the family is physically distinguishable at copy order two even though all frozen
one-copy data agree.

## 4. Minimal selector data in the restricted family

The v9.205 family is one-dimensional.

A selector that distinguishes all members must contain at least one nonconstant real datum.
The normalized SWAP excess above is injective in \(\eta\), so one real number is sufficient.

Therefore, **within this restricted family**,

\[
\boxed{
d_{\rm copy\ provenance}^{\min}=1\text{ real scalar}.
}
\]

Equivalent choices include:

- \(\eta\);
- the two-copy SWAP expectation;
- the trace distance from the iid endpoint;
- the connected-response coefficient itself, although using the response as the provenance
  definition would be circular.

The SWAP statistic is preferred as the clean source-side certificate because it is
exchange-invariant and independent of the probe response.

It still requires two identified copy slots and their joint state. Its value is not present
in the one-copy source.

## 5. Exact connection to the v9.205 processor

The retained connected-response processor has

\[
F_\eta=(1-\eta)C(\bar\rho),
\qquad
\|C(\bar\rho)\|_{\rm HS}=\frac12.
\]

Therefore

\[
\boxed{
\|F_\eta\|_{\rm HS}
=
1-\langle S_{12}\rangle_\eta.
}
\]

Equivalently,

\[
\|F_\eta\|_{\rm HS}
=
\frac{1-\eta}{2}.
\]

This relation is exact for the stipulated v9.205 family and processor.

It is not interpreted as a physical force law. Its purpose is to make the information
dependency explicit: the response depends on a copy-provenance datum absent from one-copy
source information.

## 6. Why native token count does not solve the problem

Earlier replica candidates introduced finite source-token sets and additive token count.

That structure distinguishes **how many** declared source tokens exist.

It does not determine which exchangeable quantum preparation state is assigned to those
tokens. At fixed token count \(n\), every \(R_n^{(\eta)}\) uses the same number of source
copies.

Likewise, graph relabeling and copy permutation symmetry constrain how copy labels transform,
but not which joint density is physically prepared.

Therefore:

\[
\boxed{
\text{copy count}
\neq
\text{copy provenance}.
}
\]

A count can be native or stipulated while the joint preparation relation remains free.

## 7. Why canonical disjoint tensor product is not yet a physical selector

For two specified disjoint densities \(\rho\) and \(\sigma\), the frozen tensor-product
construction supplies

\[
\rho\otimes\sigma
\]

as a valid realization.

If one **adds** the strict preparation law

\[
R_{m+n}=R_m\otimes R_n
\]

for repeated-source joins, then \(R_n=\bar\rho^{\otimes n}\) follows and \(\eta=0\).

But this is precisely the strict product/independence premise distinguished in v9.175.
Associativity and projective consistency alone do not imply it.

So iid preparation can be selected, but only by adding an independence law.

## 8. Broader hierarchy warning

The one-scalar selector is special to the restricted \(R_n^{(\eta)}\) family.

The earlier v9.175 construction gave exchangeable, deletion-consistent all-count preparation
families parameterized by a measure \(\mu\), with moments \(M_k\). It proved:

- complete two- and three-specimen states can agree while the four-specimen state differs;
- for every finite order there exist preparation families agreeing through that order but
  differing later;
- in that class the all-order moment sequence determines the preparation measure.

Therefore no fixed finite-order copy statistic is a general complete provenance object for
that broader family.

The correct general statement is:

\[
\boxed{
\text{a smaller copy-provenance parameterization must itself be justified as a physical
restriction on the preparation family}.
}
\]

Otherwise a full preparation measure or equivalent extension hierarchy may be required.

## 9. Copy provenance as a new primitive

The mathematically clean object is not necessarily a scalar.

A **copy-provenance law** can be represented abstractly as a section or relation

\[
\boxed{
\mathsf P:
\rho
\longmapsto
\{R_n\}_{n\ge1}
}
\]

through the restriction map, subject to whatever exchangeability, covariance, and
consistency rules are physically intended.

For the restricted family,

\[
\mathsf P(\bar\rho)\leftrightarrow\eta.
\]

For a more general preparation class, \(\mathsf P\) may need a measure, moment hierarchy,
or another higher-order object.

The current frozen architecture contains no canonical such section in the inspected
definitions.

## 10. Scientific disposition

The stop criterion is satisfied:

\[
\boxed{
\textbf{frozen one-copy provenance contains no selector that distinguishes the v9.205 family.}
}
\]

Thus the connected-correlation route cannot claim that its required copy preparation follows
from existing UQCF–GEM provenance.

The new required input is precisely:

\[
\boxed{
\textbf{copy provenance}.
}
\]

This does not mean copy provenance must be fundamental in nature. A future theory might derive
it from a deeper global preparation object. The current architecture does not.

This is a theorem/evidence boundary result, not a gravity breakthrough.

## 11. Verification

The checker verifies:

- exact equality of all 16 one-copy Hermitian coordinates for every tested \(\eta\);
- exact one-copy purity \(1/2\);
- the two-copy SWAP formula
  \[
  \langle S_{12}\rangle=1/2+\eta/2;
  \]
- exact recovery of \(\eta\);
- exact trace-distance law
  \[
  D(R_2^{(\eta)},R_2^{(0)})=\eta/2;
  \]
- the exact relation to the retained connected-response norm.

The native-provenance conclusion is a bounded audit of the inspected frozen definitions,
supported by the earlier v9.72, v9.96, v9.175, and replica-incidence adjudications. It is not
a theorem that no uninspected future artifact could introduce such a law.

## 12. Next gate

**v9.207 — Copy-Provenance Law Candidate Gate.**

Do not search the frozen architecture again for a selector already adjudicated absent.

Compare only explicit new copy-provenance laws:

1. strict iid/product preparation;
2. extremal/de Finetti-delta preparation;
3. finite-memory/reset preparation;
4. a genuinely global provenance object whose restrictions generate all copy counts.

Require each candidate to state:

- what object is primitive;
- how it composes;
- how restrictions work;
- what distinguishes it from the others before probe interaction;
- whether it introduces a hidden ordering variable.

No force interpretation should be attached before one candidate receives independent
justification.

# Native event synchronization and the retained-state write law

Date: 2026-11-04 America/Phoenix. Status: source recovery and scoped interpretation only.
Native receiver delivery: **NOT_DERIVED**. No scientific breakthrough, new theorem, historical rerun, or independent-review claim.

## Question and admission scope

The current target is one inherited receiver-owned effect forced by the actual event. It does not require unique microscopic dynamics, the entire joint cover, global synchronization of every historical model, exhaustive future probes, or permanent erasure.

This pass followed a previously unexamined source chain: v7.36's cross-component transition question, v7.37's native synchronization audit, and v7.38's single-subsystem search. Its explicit RSL-1 update led to the original RSL-1 specification and necessity note, plus ROC-5's primitive-origin qualifications.

The possible inherited producer was an event lift into an already identified retained component, or the RSL-1 write into its declared edge/local state. If either source supplied this receiver association, an owned effect could distinguish the preparations. The standing adversary remains W_E, but a verdict requires a typed correspondence to that benchmark. Missing owner maps, merely shared event IDs, or a supplied projection fail this source admission test. No numerical campaign is admitted by the sources recovered here.

## What the original files contain

|---|---|---|

The archive reports and their verdicts are historical source assertions. This recovery is not a fresh acceptance of their theorem, uniqueness, minimality or exhaustiveness language.

## The useful distinction: failed permanent erasure is not failed delivery

The original RSL-1 state is a vector/monoid of retained action, **not the quantum density matrix rho of the W_R/W_E benchmark**. Its increment is defined inside the finite-capacity model by

\[
x=(a\odot d^*\phi)/c,\quad
A_\sigma(x)=\int_0^x\sigma(u)\,du,\quad
\ell=c\odot[x\sigma(x)-A_\sigma(x)],
\qquad
\rho'=\rho+P_G(\ell).
\]

These are source definitions, not a new physical postulate introduced here. Their applicability to the current native event is not earned by recovery of the text.

The v7.38 argument concerns injectivity in the old retained state **at fixed increment**. Receiver acquisition instead compares different source-associated increments at the same initially blind receiver. Those are different comparisons. Within the stated vector update, for a common old state,

\[
\rho'_+-\rho'_-=P_G(\ell_+)-P_G(\ell_-).
\]

This is direct subtraction of the existing definition; it assumes neither linearity nor injectivity of P_G. It does not assert that the difference is nonzero, accessible, or produced by the P1 event. The old translation argument alone cannot decide these latter questions.

Thus the source contains an explicit conditional write rule worth accurately recording. It should not be discarded merely because it fails a permanent-erasure criterion. Equally, its symbol "retained state" does not identify that state with the designated P1 receiver.

## Why this does not yet earn a receiver consequence

The recovered specifications do not provide the following identifications:

1. A map from the current same-Genesis P1 event and the fixed preparation pair to the finite-capacity pressure/increments in this law.
2. A map identifying the law's edge/local retained state, or one of its effects, with the predesignated receiver's available output.
3. A source-backed restriction on P_G that preserves the relevant pair distinction in that owned effect.

P_G is a gauge/representation projection in this source. It is not the ancestral P1 pushforward merely because both use the letter P. The declared graph/edge state and the mathematical owner r(u) also cannot be identified by shared terminology.

ROC-5 explicitly reports that its tests used the identity/minimal edge representation and leaves nontrivial P_G open. That is stronger evidence about this source's scope than treating the old word "closed" as an inherited proof of receiver ownership.

No W_R/W_E pass/fail score is assigned: these sources do not define the needed mapping into the benchmark's Hilbert spaces, channel or designated R. Granting the stated write rule conditionally still leaves the owner association open. No conditional write/coupling experiment is proposed.

## Historical audit limitations

Inspection of the recovered programs matters:

- v7.36 stores component descriptions and missing-map flags in a table, plus a hand-built two-completion example.
- v7.37 serializes declared gate statuses and asserts that the three selected statuses equal NOT_DERIVED.
- v7.38 filters its manually populated subsystem table with qualifies() and asserts the resulting list is empty.

These scripts are not independent searches of all native source code. Their packaged logs/results show what the historical programs reported, not an exhaustive theorem that every possible receiver effect is absent. None of these programs was executed in this pass. The external source claims they summarize have not all been freshly reconstructed here.

The full SYN1–SYN5 requirements were aimed at a global permanent behavioral quotient. They are not adopted as necessary requirements for the present one-effect target. An already earned local receiver relation could suffice. Conversely, a commuting diagram whose receiver map and local update are only named does not supply them.

The RSL-1 necessity note is preserved, not recertified. This assessment uses its explicit update definition only; it does not rely on its general claim about embedding admissible commutative monoids in a vector representation.

## Preservation and verification

This additive packet contains three original ZIPs and all fifteen members, plus three original RSL/ROC source files downloaded separately. SOURCE_IDENTITIES.json records source IDs, byte counts, SHA-256 and git blob IDs. ZIP CRCs and exact extracted-member equality were checked. Original downloaded lengths match source metadata.

An initial extracted-text copy of RSL1_specification.json differed from its metadata length. The original file was then downloaded and used in this packet; no extracted-text transcription is presented as original bytes.

Publication must verify the remote blobs and an additions-only diff against PR135's prior head ef8e0c88772df4cdedf22d6c033852d552e4e5bb. The publication receipt records that readback separately. Older PR135 evidence remains unchanged. PR135 remains draft and unmerged; PR130/131 holds remain.

There is no new simulation, historical scientific rerun, Gemini request, physical gravity claim or reopening of thermal, E-alpha, Hodge, coupling-selection, or permanence campaigns. Actual atemporal occurrence remains ACCEPTED INPUT.

## Disposition

The synchronization source does not supply the desired joint event relation. RSL-1 supplies a conditional update into a declared retained action state, but the inspected originals leave its correspondence to this receiver unearned.

The useful next source would bind an existing local state/effect and its event update to this receiver before any joint realization is chosen. Neither global completeness nor permanent erasure is required merely to admit such evidence. This pass preserves an overlooked explicit update and its exact limitations; it does not close the native delivery arrow.

# Phase 57.3D11 — ROC-5 Grammar Completeness / Primitive-Origin Audit

## Verdict

`ROC5_PRIMITIVE_ORIGIN_AUDIT_PASSED_CONDITIONAL__GRAMMAR_COMPLETENESS_NOT_PROVEN`

## Diagnosis

`NO_TARGET_OR_GEOMETRY_SMUGGLING_FOUND_IN_CURRENT_HARNESS_BUT_A_C_SIGMA_AND_PG_REMAIN_CONDITIONAL_OR_OPEN_AND_FINITE_GRAMMAR_COMPLETENESS_IS_NOT_PROVEN`

## Purpose

ROC-5 audits whether the ROC-1 + ARC-1 retained operator law is hiding the answer inside its primitives, and whether ROC-4's finite grammar enumeration can be treated as complete.

## Primitive-origin result

```text
primitive-origin audit:
  PASS CONDITIONAL

forbidden primitives excluded:
  True

primitive failures:
  0

open conditional primitives:
  P_G

  a, c, sigma / constitutive family, c_ref
```

```text

  PASS

  PASS

  nontrivial projection requires formal gauge law

target K / future operator:
  PASS FORBIDDEN

metric / curvature / gravity:
  PASS FORBIDDEN

```

  false

# RSL-1 Necessity Theorem — Minimal Retained Action Ledger

## Setup

Inside RAG-0, the finite-capacity conserved-current law family is:

```text
d f_n = b_n
f_n = c_n ⊙ sigma((a_n ⊙ d*phi_n)/c_n)
```

Let:

```text
x_n = (a_n ⊙ d*phi_n)/c_n
A_sigma(x) = integral_0^x sigma(u) du
L_sigma(x) = x sigma(x) - A_sigma(x)
ell_n = c_n ⊙ L_sigma(x_n)
```

## Claim

If a retained state `rho_n` is:

```text
compact
target-blind
generated from current-slice admissible finite-capacity action residuals
closed under ordered-slice composition
not a full history replay
not a learned latent/lookup table
```

then its update must be a monoid homomorphism from slice increments into retained state:

```text
rho_{n+1} = rho_n ⊕ P_G(ell_n)
```

In the minimal vector gauge:

```text
rho_{n+1} = rho_n + P_G(ell_n)
```

## Reason

Composition of ordered slices requires:

```text
Update(Update(rho, ell_1), ell_2)
  ~ Update(rho, Compose(ell_1, ell_2))
```

Associativity plus compactness forbids dependence on the full ledger ordering except through a retained increment. Under the minimal vector representation, every admissible commutative retained-increment monoid embeds as addition up to gauge/equivalence.

## What is forced

```text
additive / monoid retained update form
current-slice finite-capacity action increment
target-blind compact state
```

## What is not forced

```text
specific sigma choice inside Sigma
projection gauge P_G
repair rule
future capacity/conductance/operator coupling
simulation proto-gravity certification
```

END EXACT DOCUMENT

# UQCF-GEM v7.36 — Frozen Transition-System Reconstruction Audit

## Verdict

\[
\boxed{
\texttt{TYPE\_OBSTRUCTION\_NO\_NATIVE\_GLOBAL\_TRANSITION\_SYSTEM\_DERIVED}
}
\]

The frozen UQCF stack does not presently define one native complete system

\[
\mathfrak M=(X,\Sigma,\delta,\mathcal A,\mathcal O).
\]

This is not an absolute impossibility theorem. A larger product state can
always be *added* mathematically. The result is a derivability theorem:

> constructing such a product and specifying how its components update requires
> synchronization/lift maps that are not supplied by the frozen model.

## 1. The frozen components act on different types

The causal governor operates on a ledger-prefix state containing objects such
as active event IDs, source provenance, sequence checks, entropy checks and
damage/repair counts.

Genesis updates an append-only provenance/history root.

V1494 constructs `Event2D` records and then computes geometry/constraint
diagnostics from complete regime-specific traces.

V1689.9 instead declares

\[
A_e:G_p\to G_p'
\]

on

\[
G_p=\langle B_p,O3_p,H4^\perp_p,S_p,\Pi_p,\oplus_p\rangle.
\]

The earlier provenance-functor design explicitly records that object maps,
morphisms, identities, composition, refinement naturality and gauge uniqueness
were not yet satisfied.

Therefore these are not currently projections of a single frozen native state
with one common event update.

## 2. What a global transition system would require

For each component state \(X_i\), a genuine global state \(X\) would require a
projection

\[
p_i:X\to X_i.
\]

Every native event \(e\) would need compatible updates satisfying

\[
\boxed{
p_i\circ\delta_e
=
\delta^i_e\circ p_i.
}
\]

No frozen construction supplies these commuting squares simultaneously for:

- the governor ledger;
- Genesis provenance state;
- generated retained algebra;
- geometry/event state;
- observer/probe state.

In particular there is no frozen rule proving that an event called
`branch_prune` in the generated-algebra grammar is the same typed morphism as
a disruption/loss event in the empirical governor trace.

## 3. The exact synchronization gap

The missing glue decomposes into five requirements.

### G1 — Common event identity

A native rule must identify when events appearing in the governor, Genesis
ledger, `Event2D` trace, and \(\Pi_p\) ledger are the same event.

### G2 — Common state maps

There must be typed maps between the ledger/history state, generated algebra,
provenance root, and native observer state.

### G3 — Event lift

For every event, the model must specify compatible updates on all relevant
components.

### G4 — Counterfactual totality

These updates must be defined on every admissible counterfactual state/event
pair, not only on the realized trace used by a harness.

### G5 — Exhaustive output semantics

The model must specify which outputs/probes are behaviorally complete.

Without G1-G5, the v7.35 behavioral quotient cannot be evaluated.

## 4. Completion-nonuniqueness null

There are at least two completions consistent with the same presently visible
frozen interfaces.

After a `branch_prune`, both completions can expose the same:

- updated ledger;
- updated Genesis root;
- visible generated algebra with the branch suppressed.

Completion A also destroys a hidden recoverability token.

Completion B retains it so a future event may restore the branch.

The current frozen interfaces cannot distinguish those completions.

Thus they induce different permanent behavioral equivalence while agreeing on
the declared present component outputs.

Therefore:

\[
\boxed{
\text{frozen component actions}
\not\Rightarrow
\text{unique global }\delta_e.
}
\]

## 5. Why V1689.9 does not close the gap

V1689.9 is valuable because it explicitly declares generated-algebra event
actions. But it is frozen as an algebra-action protocol and does not supply the
normalized state-space/counterfactual semantics required by the behavioral
quotient.

Earlier v7.32 already identified the missing state-space domain/codomain,
normalization, positivity/channel structure, quotient fibers, and linkage to
the coarse retained quotient.

That negative result must be preserved.

## 6. Why the common-generator branch does not close it

V1689.14 later proposed one common generated-algebra generator \(A_{pq}\), but
that object was itself frozen as a protocol requiring derivation. It does not
retroactively provide a total native transition system for every event and
every counterfactual retained state.

Likewise, the Phase 51 provenance-functor instrumentation explicitly left the
category/functor requirements unsatisfied.

## Final status

\[
\boxed{
\begin{array}{ll}
\text{common retained state }X & \mathbf{NOT\ DERIVED}\\
\text{common event alphabet }\Sigma & \mathbf{PARTIAL/UNSYNCHRONIZED}\\
\text{global event transition }\delta & \mathbf{NOT\ DERIVED}\\
\text{counterfactual admissibility }\mathcal A & \mathbf{NOT\ COMPLETE}\\
\text{exhaustive output semantics }\mathcal O & \mathbf{NOT\ DERIVED}\\
\text{strict behavioral merge test} & \mathbf{NOT\ YET\ TYPEABLE}
\end{array}
}
\]

No scientific breakthrough is claimed.

## Next gate

\[
\boxed{
\textbf{v7.37 — Native Event Synchronization Derive-or-No-Go Audit}
}
\]

Search the frozen model specifically for G1-G3: a native event identity,
cross-component state maps, and a commuting event lift.

If those are absent, freeze G1-G5 as the minimal additional semantics. Do not
manufacture a Cartesian-product state and call it native.

END EXACT DOCUMENT

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
UQCF-GEM v7.36 — Frozen Transition-System Reconstruction Audit

Question
--------
Do the already-frozen UQCF components define one common native partial
transition/output system

    M = (X, Sigma, delta, A, O)

without adding synchronization/lift semantics?

Frozen type contracts represented here
--------------------------------------
1. causal governor:
   operates on a supplied event-prefix ledger state and validates events.

2. Genesis provenance:
   appends a cryptographic/provenance root to a realized history prefix.

3. V1494 stress trace:
   constructs typed Event2D records and downstream geometry diagnostics.

4. V1689.9:
   declares event action A_e:G_p -> G_p' on a generated retained algebra and
   a linearized action rho_e=dA_e.

5. Phase 51.1 event functor design:
   explicitly leaves object/morphism maps, identity, composition, refinement,
   and gauge uniqueness unsatisfied.

A single transition system could always be ADDED by taking a product state
and specifying missing component update/lift maps. But that product/glue is
not DERIVED by the frozen stack.

The audit therefore distinguishes:
- existence of possible completions;
- derivability/uniqueness from the frozen declarations.

Verdict
-------
TYPE_OBSTRUCTION_NO_NATIVE_GLOBAL_TRANSITION_SYSTEM_DERIVED

The exact missing object is a synchronization natural transformation / event
lift assigning every native event a compatible update on every retained
component.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
import json


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Component:
    name: str
    state_type: str
    event_input: str
    update_kind: str
    total_counterfactual_update_declared: bool
    output_semantics_declared: bool
    frozen_status: str


COMPONENTS = [
    Component(
        name="V1472_causal_governor",
        state_type="ledger prefix state L=(active_event_ids, source_seen, provenance, counters, entropy/order checks)",
        event_input="supplied Event record",
        update_kind="prefix validation + append event id/counters",
        total_counterfactual_update_declared=False,
        output_semantics_declared=False,
        frozen_status="validator on realized trace",
    ),
    Component(
        name="Genesis_provenance",
        state_type="append-only provenance root/history R",
        event_input="realized event record",
        update_kind="R_{k+1}=H(R_k,event_{k+1},...)",
        total_counterfactual_update_declared=False,
        output_semantics_declared=False,
        frozen_status="record-integrity engine",
    ),
    Component(
        name="V1494_Event2D_geometry",
        state_type="Event2D records + geometry/constraint diagnostic arrays",
        event_input="regime-specific generated Event2D trace",
        update_kind="trace construction then downstream field diagnostics",
        total_counterfactual_update_declared=False,
        output_semantics_declared=False,
        frozen_status="valid stress-regime diagnostic",
    ),
    Component(
        name="V1689_9_generated_algebra_action",
        state_type="generated retained algebra G_p=<B,O3,H4,S,Pi,oplus>",
        event_input="e in provenance ledger Pi_p",
        update_kind="declared A_e:G_p->G_p' and linearized rho_e=dA_e",
        total_counterfactual_update_declared=False,
        output_semantics_declared=False,
        frozen_status="FROZEN_NO_EXECUTION / algebra action representation",
    ),
    Component(
        name="Phase51_1_event_functor",
        state_type="provenance category objects/arrows -> chain support",
        event_input="retained event arrows",
        update_kind="proposed F0/F1/Phi functor",
        total_counterfactual_update_declared=False,
        output_semantics_declared=False,
        frozen_status="category/functor requirements explicitly unsatisfied",
    ),
]


def required_global_contract():
    return {
        "X": "one common retained state type",
        "Sigma": "one common event alphabet with typed parameters",
        "delta": "for every admissible event, a native partial map delta_e:X->X",
        "A": "admissibility predicate on arbitrary counterfactual states/events",
        "O": "exhaustive native distinguishability/probe map",
        "sync": "compatibility maps making component projections commute with delta_e",
    }


def component_projection_square(component: Component):
    """
    For a genuine global state X with projection p_i:X->X_i, every event e
    needs a local update delta^i_e such that

         p_i(delta_e(x)) = delta^i_e(p_i(x))

    whenever e is admissible.

    The current frozen stack does not provide these squares simultaneously.
    """
    return {
        "component": component.name,
        "projection_needed": f"p_{component.name}: X -> {component.state_type}",
        "commuting_square_needed": "p_i ∘ delta_e = delta^i_e ∘ p_i",
        "local_total_counterfactual_delta_declared": component.total_counterfactual_update_declared,
        "exhaustive_output_declared": component.output_semantics_declared,
        "square_closed_from_frozen_data": False,
    }


def two_completion_null():
    """
    Same frozen visible component interfaces, different hidden synchronization.

    This proves the frozen interfaces do not uniquely determine global delta.
    """
    visible_before = {
        "ledger": ("active:e0",),
        "genesis_root": "r0",
        "algebra_visible": ("branch:b",),
    }
    event = "branch_prune:b"

    # Completion A: prune also deletes hidden recoverability token.
    completion_A_after = {
        "ledger": ("active:e0", "active:e1"),
        "genesis_root": "r1",
        "algebra_visible": tuple(),
        "hidden_recovery_token": None,
    }

    # Completion B: identical frozen visible updates, hidden recovery token retained.
    completion_B_after = {
        "ledger": ("active:e0", "active:e1"),
        "genesis_root": "r1",
        "algebra_visible": tuple(),
        "hidden_recovery_token": "b",
    }

    visible_keys = ("ledger", "genesis_root", "algebra_visible")
    same_visible = all(
        completion_A_after[k] == completion_B_after[k] for k in visible_keys
    )
    hidden_differs = (
        completion_A_after["hidden_recovery_token"]
        != completion_B_after["hidden_recovery_token"]
    )

    return {
        "event": event,
        "same_frozen_visible_component_updates": same_visible,
        "different_hidden_recoverability_semantics": hidden_differs,
        "completion_A_permanent": True,
        "completion_B_permanent": False,
        "conclusion": "Frozen component interfaces do not uniquely determine a global event transition or permanence semantics.",
    }


def minimal_missing_glue():
    return {
        "G1_common_event_identity": (
            "A rule identifying when a governor event, Genesis ledger event, "
            "Event2D event, and Pi_p algebra event are the same native event."
        ),
        "G2_common_state_embeddings": (
            "Typed projections/embeddings between the ledger/history state, "
            "generated algebra G_p, geometry/observer state, and provenance root."
        ),
        "G3_event_lift": (
            "For each native event e, compatible component updates delta^i_e "
            "with p_i∘delta_e=delta^i_e∘p_i."
        ),
        "G4_counterfactual_totality": (
            "The event lift must be defined for every admissible counterfactual "
            "state/event pair, not merely realized traces."
        ),
        "G5_output_exhaustivity": (
            "One native O specifying all behaviorally relevant probes/outcomes."
        ),
    }


def main():
    out = Path("v7_36_outputs")
    out.mkdir(exist_ok=True)

    squares = [component_projection_square(c) for c in COMPONENTS]
    null = two_completion_null()

    assert all(not s["square_closed_from_frozen_data"] for s in squares)
    assert null["same_frozen_visible_component_updates"]
    assert null["different_hidden_recoverability_semantics"]

    payload = {
        "version": "v7.36",
        "title": "Frozen Transition-System Reconstruction Audit",
        "verdict": "TYPE_OBSTRUCTION_NO_NATIVE_GLOBAL_TRANSITION_SYSTEM_DERIVED",
        "global_contract": required_global_contract(),
        "component_contracts": [c.__dict__ for c in COMPONENTS],
        "commuting_square_audit": squares,
        "completion_nonuniqueness_null": null,
        "minimal_missing_glue": minimal_missing_glue(),
        "formal_result": {
            "absolute_existence_no_go": False,
            "reason": (
                "A product-state completion can always be stipulated in principle; "
                "the no-go is non-derivability from frozen declarations, not logical impossibility."
            ),
            "native_global_X_derived": False,
            "native_global_delta_derived": False,
            "native_exhaustive_A_derived": False,
            "native_exhaustive_O_derived": False,
            "strict_behavioral_merge_test_currently_typeable": False,
        },
        "scientific_breakthrough": False,
        "next_gate": (
            "v7.37 — Native Event Synchronization Derive-or-No-Go Audit: search "
            "the frozen stack for a native common event identity and commuting "
            "event lift. If absent, freeze G1-G5 as the exact minimal additional semantics."
        ),
    }

    (out / "v7_36_results.json").write_text(json.dumps(payload, indent=2))

    print("UQCF-GEM v7.36 — FROZEN TRANSITION-SYSTEM RECONSTRUCTION AUDIT")
    print("VERDICT:", payload["verdict"])
    print()
    print("Global X derived:", payload["formal_result"]["native_global_X_derived"])
    print("Global delta derived:", payload["formal_result"]["native_global_delta_derived"])
    print("Exhaustive A derived:", payload["formal_result"]["native_exhaustive_A_derived"])
    print("Exhaustive O derived:", payload["formal_result"]["native_exhaustive_O_derived"])
    print("Strict behavioral merge currently typeable:",
          payload["formal_result"]["strict_behavioral_merge_test_currently_typeable"])
    print()
    print("Completion nonuniqueness null:")
    print("  same frozen visible updates:",
          null["same_frozen_visible_component_updates"])
    print("  different permanence semantics:",
          null["different_hidden_recoverability_semantics"])


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

# UQCF-GEM v7.37 — Native Event Synchronization Derive-or-No-Go Audit

## Verdict

\[
\boxed{
\texttt{NO\_NATIVE\_EVENT\_SYNCHRONIZATION\_FUNCTOR\_DERIVED}
}
\]

The v7.36 typing obstruction cannot be repaired from already-frozen
synchronization structure, because the relevant synchronization structure was
itself explicitly left open in earlier branches.

The provenance-chain program recorded the object map and arrow map as missing.
The later F1 instrumentation still marked `repair_or_prune_transition` as
missing a chain-map rule, with identity, composition, refinement naturality,
and gauge uniqueness not certified.

The common-generator branch also does not close the problem. \(A_{pq}\) was
introduced precisely because event current and transport needed a common
generator, but the branch postmortem states that the clean-room generator was
not first-principles selected and that the selection principle remained
missing.

Thus shared event labels and IDs are only schemas. They do not supply a
commuting native event morphism.

## Minimal extra synchronization semantics

The required additions can now be frozen as:

- **SYN1** — one typed common event identity across the governor, Genesis,
  Event2D, generated-algebra and observer layers;
- **SYN2** — typed object maps from one retained state into each layer;
- **SYN3** — a native event arrow map preserving identities and composition;
- **SYN4** — counterfactual totality for every admissible event string;
- **SYN5** — exhaustive native output/probe semantics.

These are not derived by the frozen stack.

## Consequence

The permanent behavioral merge test remains not globally typeable. Therefore
the first breakthrough arrow

\[
\text{frozen event dynamics}
\Rightarrow
\text{permanent nontrivial quotient}
\]

has not yet been established.

## Next gate

The shortest remaining route is narrower:

\[
\boxed{
\textbf{v7.38 — Single-Subsystem Permanent-Merge Search}
}
\]

Instead of demanding a globally synchronized state immediately, search each
already-typed frozen subsystem independently for a complete local
\((X,\Sigma,\delta,\mathcal A,\mathcal O)\) and a non-injective behavioral
event.

If one exists, permanent pruning can be derived locally first and only then
lifted into the retained bridge.

END EXACT DOCUMENT

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
UQCF-GEM v7.37 — Native Event Synchronization Derive-or-No-Go Audit

Target:
Can the frozen stack derive G1-G3 from v7.36?
  G1 common event identity
  G2 common state maps
  G3 commuting event lift

Verdict:
NO_NATIVE_EVENT_SYNCHRONIZATION_FUNCTOR_DERIVED

The strongest existing candidate is the provenance functor / common-generator
program, but frozen artifacts explicitly record the required object map,
arrow map, composition, refinement naturality, and canonical selection as
missing/not certified.

Therefore synchronization cannot be inferred from shared labels such as
event_id, repair, branch_prune, or provenance. Shared field names provide
schema correspondence, not a commuting transition law.
"""

from pathlib import Path
import json

def main():
    out = Path("v7_37_outputs")
    out.mkdir(exist_ok=True)

    gates = {
        "G1_common_event_identity": {
            "status": "NOT_DERIVED",
            "reason": (
                "Event schemas overlap, but no frozen equivalence/identity rule "
                "proves that governor, Event2D, Genesis, and Pi_p records with "
                "related labels denote one morphism."
            )
        },
        "G2_common_state_maps": {
            "status": "NOT_DERIVED",
            "reason": (
                "No frozen F0/object map or typed projections connect ledger "
                "state, generated algebra, provenance root, geometry state, and "
                "behavioral observer state."
            )
        },
        "G3_commuting_event_lift": {
            "status": "NOT_DERIVED",
            "reason": (
                "Phase 51.1 explicitly records repair_or_prune_transition as "
                "missing its F1 chain-map rule; identity, composition, and "
                "refinement naturality remain uncertified."
            )
        },
        "common_generator_candidate": {
            "status": "INSUFFICIENT",
            "reason": (
                "A_pq was proposed as common generated-algebra generator but "
                "was frozen as a protocol/scaffold; its first-principles "
                "selection/compatibility was not established."
            )
        },
        "shared_event_id_fields": {
            "status": "SCHEMA_ONLY",
            "reason": (
                "Common names/IDs can correlate records but do not define "
                "state transport, composition, or counterfactual updates."
            )
        }
    }

    all_core_missing = all(
        gates[k]["status"] == "NOT_DERIVED"
        for k in ("G1_common_event_identity","G2_common_state_maps","G3_commuting_event_lift")
    )
    assert all_core_missing

    payload = {
        "version": "v7.37",
        "verdict": "NO_NATIVE_EVENT_SYNCHRONIZATION_FUNCTOR_DERIVED",
        "gates": gates,
        "minimal_extra_semantics_now_frozen": {
            "SYN1": "typed common event object / identity relation across frozen subsystems",
            "SYN2": "typed object maps from one retained state to each subsystem state",
            "SYN3": "event arrow map/lift preserving identity and composition",
            "SYN4": "counterfactual totality on all admissible event strings",
            "SYN5": "exhaustive native output/probe functor",
        },
        "consequence": {
            "behavioral_merge_test_available": False,
            "NPPS_native_derivation": False,
            "counting_entropy_event_bridge": False,
            "scientific_breakthrough": False,
        },
        "next_gate": (
            "v7.38 — Search for a native non-injective map already present in any "
            "single fully typed subsystem. If one subsystem independently has "
            "complete state, transition, admissibility, and output semantics, "
            "permanent pruning could be derived locally before global synchronization."
        )
    }

    (out / "v7_37_results.json").write_text(json.dumps(payload, indent=2))
    print("UQCF-GEM v7.37 — NATIVE EVENT SYNCHRONIZATION DERIVE-OR-NO-GO")
    print("VERDICT:", payload["verdict"])
    for k,v in gates.items():
        print(k, ":", v["status"])

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

# UQCF-GEM v7.38 — Single-Subsystem Permanent-Merge Search

## Verdict

\[
\boxed{
\texttt{NO\_FROZEN\_SINGLE\_SUBSYSTEM\_DERIVES\_NATIVE\_PERMANENT\_BEHAVIORAL\_MERGE}
}
\]

The global synchronization route failed in v7.36-v7.37. v7.38 therefore
tested whether any already-typed frozen subsystem independently contains the
complete behavioral ingredients needed to derive permanent pruning.

None does.

## Criteria

A qualifying subsystem must contain:

1. a typed state space \(X\);
2. a native transition \(\delta\) on arbitrary admissible state/event pairs;
3. complete counterfactual admissibility \(\mathcal A\);
4. exhaustive native output/probe semantics \(\mathcal O\);
5. a native event that strictly merges previously distinct behavioral classes.

## Strongest near miss: V1472

The causal governor has the strongest executable internal state and update
logic. It maintains ledger variables such as active event IDs, provenance and
sequence/entropy counters.

But the governor is a realized-prefix validator. Its diagnostics are not
declared to be the exhaustive native outputs by which retained states are
distinguished.

Therefore even if two different histories collapse to the same internal
validator memory, that would establish only an implementation-level
validator-state quotient. It would not establish permanent erasure of UQCF
retained information.

## Genesis

Genesis has an append-only root update and therefore a typed record state.

But collision/cardinality arguments about a finite hash are not a legitimate
pruning theorem. They would concern the implementation of the provenance
commitment, not a native event that permanently removes a retained
distinction.

No exhaustive behavioral output semantics is declared.

## V1494

`Event2D` is strongly typed, but the code constructs complete
regime-specific traces and then evaluates geometry/constraint diagnostics.
It does not expose one transition function on arbitrary retained states and
counterfactual events.

## V1689.9

The generated-algebra branch has the most relevant event vocabulary, including
`branch_prune`, but its action is frozen as an algebra-action protocol:

\[
A_e:G_p\to G_p'.
\]

The protocol does not close normalized state semantics, quotient fibers,
counterfactual admissibility, or exhaustive outputs.

## RSL-1, ROC-1 and ARC-1

These are useful structural controls but not permanent pruning:

- RSL-1 is a translation for fixed increment and is invertible by subtraction;
- ROC-1 uses \(e^{-r}>0\) at finite burden, so suppression remains injective;
- ARC-1 restores accessibility.

Hence none supplies a strict native quotient.

## Final consequence

The derive-first search for permanent pruning in the frozen event stack is now
exhausted without success.

This is a meaningful stop:

\[
\boxed{
\text{the next permanent-pruning step would be model addition, not derivation}.
}
\]

No scientific breakthrough is claimed.

The research should now choose explicitly between two paths:

1. **Model-extension path:** introduce synchronization and exhaustive
   counterfactual-output semantics as new axioms, then test them aggressively.

2. **Axiom-invariance path:** avoid selecting permanence semantics and ask
   whether any downstream recoverability/modular/continuum law is invariant
   over the entire class of admissible completions. A completion-invariant law
   would be genuinely model-native despite the underdetermination.

END EXACT DOCUMENT
