# Native incidence: existing subsystem identity does not assign the event's receiver

Date: 2026-11-04, America/Phoenix.
Research baseline: 1a18308a026c06882b428fbe201b4634c34dfd8f.
Publication parent: b5dc90e527270ea018e6cbae9e01a5c1dd1d8137.
Status: bounded source recovery and reconciliation. Native delivery NOT_DERIVED.

## Question and inspected object

Aaron's question is what native object singles out R before constructing W_R or W_E. The historical v9.72 report explicitly says fixed graph incidence and subsystem factors are present. That positive source statement deserved inspection: it must not be erased by a blanket claim that the archive contains no native subsystem identities.

This packet follows v9.72's three named source dependencies in full. It preserves four complete text transcriptions, with their original qualifications, dates and historical assertions. These are source evidence, not new independent certification of their theorems or historical executions.

## What is already defined

v9.50 §1 fixes a graph G, subsystem factors H_i, a global density domain D_G, and an edge-marginal map M_E. v9.72 §§1–3 uses those factors, subsystem restriction and disjoint product composition. The fixed graph can therefore name a subsystem before any new dilation is selected.

That is a genuine mathematical subsystem identity inside this historical architecture. The reports do not identify its factors with the current P1 carrier's retained owners or establish that an actual pruning event delivers a source distinction to one of their available records. The graph, the admissible state domain and the restriction map must not be silently replaced by the later FCV lineage/grade objects.

## Trace of the candidate source-to-incidence operation

| Source | Explicit operation or object | Source's own scope |
| --- | --- | --- |
| v9.50 §1 and §3 | Fixed marginal map M_E:D_G -> edge densities; source-conditioned subsets and state transformations | The compatibility image is fixed when its domain and map are fixed. Reachability and conditioning are different questions from changing that image. |
| Provenance Multiplicity §§1–2, 6–7 | A declared provenance monoid/congruence, additive count, and a separately justified sector relation B_p | Composition and multiplicity do not supply the provenance-to-quantum relation. Its decoupled example has only the explicitly stated scope. |
| Replica Incidence §§1–4, 8 | One extra partner slot per source token, copy-role identifications, and equal pair marginals | Explicit NEW-AXIOM candidate; changing incidence and matching obligations are postulated together. It is not an inherited event writer. |
| v9.72 §§3–7 | Product composition on disjoint systems; conjunction of overlapping marginal specifications | Disjoint products do not add interaction; overlap agreement does not guarantee a joint realization. No source-to-new-incidence assignment is established by these definitions. |

The strongest positive relation in this packet is the replica candidate's shared-interface constraint. It acts on joint states, but the original text expressly supplies the source-to-slot and equal-marginal rules. Its feasibility bound does not choose a post-event state, an actual event, or a receiver-owned observation. No replica or shareability experiment is opened here.

## Present receiver-delivery disposition

1. **Native ownership:** fixed factors and graph incidence are available inside the v9 model. The additional map from the current event/retained owner to a particular factor's accessible output is not supplied by the inspected operation.
2. **Joint-event asymmetry:** overlapping obligations can be genuinely joint. Here the source-dependent obligations occur in an explicitly postulated extension; the fixed architecture supplies no event action assigning them.
3. **Realization-independent receiver consequence:** the inspected reports establish no current P1 receiver-owned output effect. A constraint on feasible joint states is not, by itself, a necessary change at the designated receiver.
4. **W_R/W_E:** no inherited identification types the v9 factors/edge marginals as the benchmark's E/R factors. Neither realization is scored. A source-to-slot assignment cannot be imported merely because it would exclude the blind example.
5. **Accepted input:** actual atemporal measurement/pruning occurrence remains accepted. Nothing here restarts an occurrence, probability-selection or fundamental-time derivation.

This is not a theorem that the whole archive is incapable of selecting a receiver. It is a four-document source trace. The fixed-image result concerns deformation of the admissibility arena; it must not be promoted into a theorem against every state change or receiver interaction inside that arena. The replica obstruction concerns its stated domain and preservation contract.

## Already parked source-local route

The retrieval also surfaced the v9.34 Minimal Selector and v9.35 Commuting Pruning-Semilattice reports, which were read completely. The agent thread already treats these in T209/T210/T219–T221. T219-gpt requires a new immutable source connecting that operation to the P1 carrier and an owned observation, or an explicit user retarget, before reopening its campaign.

Those conditions are not met by this recovery. The source-local Lüders map's unchanged exterior restriction and the conditional instrument discussion are not new findings here. They are not republished as a new candidate, and no instrument-uniqueness, ordering or dephasing test is launched.

## Evidence and limits

The four primary reports were resolved by exact titles and read completely. SOURCE_IDENTITIES.json records Library identities, read coverage, reported byte lengths, transcription byte lengths, SHA-256 and Git-blob identities. A terminating LF is added only where the extracted read text lacks one. Matching a reported length after that convention is not original-byte verification; no source ZIP was downloaded for this packet. The v9.50 transcription is 12,552 bytes versus 12,553 in the returned metadata; that one-byte discrepancy is recorded and unresolved, rather than padded away. The other three transcription lengths match the reported lengths.

The reports are preserved unchanged apart from that terminal-LF convention. Their words “certified,” “native,” “theorem,” or “new” remain historical assertions with their original scope. No report's external paper, old implementation, numerical result or review archive was independently reconstructed in this pass.

No scientific module was executed; there is no new numerical campaign, Gemini call, physical postulate, stage certification or merge. Publication readback checks establish source-text identity, not mathematical or physical truth.

## Useful re-entry evidence

A subsequent lead needs an existing source operation relating an actual event to an already identified owner's retained/available component. It may be non-Hilbert and may determine only one effect. It need not select a unique microscopic interaction or a full receiver state.

Do not start a replica-slot, source-count, overlap-gluing or source-local dephasing campaign from these documents. Their explicit missing assignments remain missing. Existing conditional results and the thermal closeout are unchanged.

# UQCF-GEM v9.50 — Source-Dependent Admissibility Deformation Existence Gate

## Status

**Theorem-first branch audit. No ensemble simulation was released.**

Certified classification:

\[
\boxed{
\texttt{NONTRIVIAL\_SOURCE\_DEPENDENT\_ADMISSIBILITY\_REQUIRES\_A\_NEW\_KINEMATIC\_PRIMITIVE}
}
\]

This is an exact statement about the frozen finite-dimensional UQCF-GEM admissibility architecture. It is not a theorem that gravity cannot exist before pruning.

---

## 1. Frozen architecture

Let

\[
\mathcal H_G=\bigotimes_{i\in V}\mathcal H_i,
\qquad
\mathcal D_G=\{X=X^\dagger:X\succeq0,\ \operatorname{Tr}X=1\},
\]

and let

\[
\mathsf M_E:\mathcal D_G\longrightarrow
\prod_{e\in E}\mathcal D(\mathcal H_e)
\]

be the fixed edge-marginal map. The compatibility arena is

\[
\mathcal A_G=\mathsf M_E(\mathcal D_G).
\]

The gate asks whether a source label \(s\) can produce a nonconstant family

\[
\mathcal A_G(s)
\]

without adding a source-dependent state cone, global constraint, Hilbert-space structure, tensor factorization, marginal/gluing map, or selection law.

---

## 2. Fixed-image theorem

### Theorem 1 — Parameter-absence / fixed-image theorem

If \(\mathcal D_G\) and \(\mathsf M_E\) are fixed, then the set of globally compatible visible data is fixed:

\[
\boxed{\mathcal A_G=\mathsf M_E(\mathcal D_G).}
\]

An external label \(s\) that has no representation in either the domain or the map cannot change this image.

### Proof

The image of a fixed set under a fixed map is a fixed set. A nonconstant source-indexed image must therefore have the form

\[
\mathcal A_G(s)=\mathsf M_{E,s}(\mathcal D_{G,s}),
\]

with at least one of \(\mathcal D_{G,s}\) or \(\mathsf M_{E,s}\) nonconstant, or else it is not a deformation of the frozen compatibility construction. \(\square\)

This elementary theorem is decisive because all current source constructions act on states, weights, objectives, or selected fibers while retaining \(\mathcal D_G\) and \(\mathsf M_E\).

---

## 3. Exhaustive classification inside the frozen architecture

Let \(s\) enter a construction while \(\mathcal D_G\) and \(\mathsf M_E\) remain fixed.

### 3.1 Source-conditioned predicate

For a predicate \(P_s(X)\), define

\[
\mathcal D_{G|s}=\{X\in\mathcal D_G:P_s(X)\},
\qquad
\mathcal A_{G|s}=\mathsf M_E(\mathcal D_{G|s}).
\]

Then

\[
\mathcal A_{G|s}\subseteq\mathcal A_G.
\]

This is state selection or conditioning. The compatibility arena has not changed; a subset has been chosen. Treating this subset as the new arena adds the predicate \(P_s\) as a hard source-dependent constraint.

### 3.2 State transformation

For any map \(T_s:\mathcal D_G\to\mathcal D_G\),

\[
\mathcal R_s=\mathsf M_E(T_s(\mathcal D_G))\subseteq\mathcal A_G.
\]

This is a reachable set inside the existing arena. It is not a deformation of global compatibility. v9.49 additionally proved that a deterministic trace-preserving operation localized to a source subsystem leaves the entire exterior reduced state unchanged.

### 3.3 Reweighting or measure change

A density \(w_s(X)\) changes probabilities on \(\mathcal D_G\). If it has full support, the support of compatible visible data remains \(\mathcal A_G\). If it has smaller support, it implements selection. No new arena follows from weighting alone.

### 3.4 Objective change

Replacing an objective \(J(X)\) by \(J_s(X)\) changes a preferred optimizer, not feasibility:

\[
\operatorname*{argmin}_{X\in\mathcal D_G}J_s(X)
\quad\text{versus}\quad
\mathcal D_G.
\]

An energy term, completion burden, Hodge action, or other selector cannot become an admissibility law unless a new rule declares nonoptimizers forbidden.

### Classification theorem

Within the frozen architecture, all source-indexed constructions of these types reduce to:

\[
\boxed{
\text{selection},\quad
\text{reachability},\quad
\text{reweighting},\quad
\text{or objective choice}.
}
\]

None is a nontrivial deformation of \(\mathcal A_G\).

---

## 4. Covariance cannot supply the missing source law

Covariance constrains a source-dependent construction after a source action has been specified. It does not create that action.

Suppose a symmetry group \(K\) acts on the kinematic data and on source labels. A covariant family would obey schematically

\[
\mathcal A_G(k\cdot s)=k\cdot\mathcal A_G(s).
\]

To obtain a nonconstant family, the theory still needs:

1. a representation of source labels;
2. a seed deformation or source-indexed constraint;
3. an action on the feasible global states or the compatibility map.

These are additional data. Covariance can reject arbitrary additions, but cannot derive a nonzero deformation from a label absent from the frozen kinematics.

---

## 5. Can sourcehood be a defect of global realizability?

This is the strongest constructive alternative suggested by v9.49.

### 5.1 Absolute compatibility defect

For visible data \(y\), the intrinsic feasibility question is

\[
y\in\mathcal A_G\quad\Longleftrightarrow\quad
\exists X\in\mathcal D_G:\mathsf M_E(X)=y.
\]

The metric-free obstruction is binary:

\[
\delta_G(y)=
\begin{cases}
0,&y\in\mathcal A_G,\\
1,&y\notin\mathcal A_G.
\end{cases}
\]

Every visible configuration produced by a valid global state has \(\delta_G=0\). Therefore a nonzero absolute compatibility defect cannot simultaneously be a property of a globally compatible realization in the frozen theory.

Quantitative distance from \(\mathcal A_G\) additionally requires a norm, divergence, or cost. A dual infeasibility witness certifies exterior data that have no global realization; it does not by itself define a physical source inside a valid realization.

### 5.2 Relative defect

A nonzero defect inside \(\mathcal A_G\) can be defined relative to a stricter reference class \(\mathcal C_0\subsetneq\mathcal D_G\):

\[
\delta_{\mathcal C_0}(y)=0
\quad\Longleftrightarrow\quad
y\in\mathsf M_E(\mathcal C_0).
\]

But \(\mathcal C_0\), its metric or obstruction theory, and the rule identifying its defect with sourcehood are new primitives unless independently derived.

### 5.3 Fiber singularity and topology

The realization fiber

\[
\mathfrak F(y)=\{X\in\mathcal D_G:\mathsf M_E(X)=y\}
\]

can have intrinsic rank strata, isotropy, dimension, singularities, or topology. These may define genuine global invariants. They still do not supply a source label or a source-composition law by themselves. Calling a particular fiber feature a source requires a derived equivalence between that feature, a local signature, and a rule for combining multiple defects.

### Defect-source adjudication

The current theory can define incompatibility and fiber structure. It cannot derive **sourcehood** as a nonzero defect of a compatible state without introducing a reference class, extended state object, altered gluing law, or defect variable.

Thus the proposed reversal

\[
\text{global defect}\to\text{local source signature}\to\text{global response}
\]

is logically viable only after the first arrow is supplied by new mathematics. It is not presently a consequence of \((\mathcal D_G,\mathsf M_E)\).

---

## 6. Minimal new primitive

The weakest mathematically honest addition is a **source representation on admissibility data**.

Let \((\mathsf S,\oplus,0)\) be a source-composition monoid. A source law must assign

\[
s\longmapsto \mathfrak K_s,
\qquad
\mathfrak K_s=(\mathcal H_{G,s},\mathcal C_{G,s},\mathcal D_{G,s},\mathsf M_{E,s}),
\]

with

\[
\mathcal A_G(s)=\mathsf M_{E,s}(\mathcal D_{G,s}),
\qquad
\mathfrak K_0=\mathfrak K.
\]

At least one nontrivial component is necessary:

| Possible addition | What it changes | Main burden |
|---|---|---|
| Source-indexed global constraint \(\mathcal D_{G,s}\subseteq\mathcal D_G\) | Allowed global realizations | Must not be disguised conditioning |
| Source-indexed positive cone \(\mathcal C_{G,s}\) | Meaning of positivity/physical state | Must preserve probability and composition |
| Source-indexed marginal or gluing map \(\mathsf M_{E,s}\) | How global and local data correspond | Must be canonical and subsystem-consistent |
| Source-indexed factorization \(\mathcal H_{G,s}\) | Subsystem structure | Must derive local identities and comparison maps |
| Extended global object \((X,z)\) with defect variable \(z\) | Adds latent compatibility data | Must derive observables and eliminate arbitrary gauge |

The logically smallest addition is a source-indexed feasible global set plus a principled composition law. The physically stronger option is a source action on the gluing/compatibility structure from which those sets follow.

---

## 7. Composition gate

A one-source deformation is insufficient. Independent sources need a coherent composition rule. Minimally:

\[
\mathfrak K_0=\mathfrak K,
\qquad
\mathfrak K_{s\oplus t}\simeq
\mathfrak K_s\star\mathfrak K_t,
\]

where \(\star\) must be associative and covariant, and should be symmetric for operationally independent sources.

Examples illustrate the burden:

- Hard independent constraints suggest \(\mathcal D_{s\oplus t}=\mathcal D_s\cap\mathcal D_t\), but this is idempotent and does not automatically represent additive source strength.
- Channel composition gives \(T_{s\oplus t}=T_s\circ T_t\), but v9.49 shows local CPTP actions do not deform exterior compatibility.
- Map or cone deformation can be nontrivial, but requires a canonical rule for composing deformations and comparing their domains.

Therefore source composition is not a later phenomenological detail. It is part of the missing primitive's definition.

---

## 8. Gate adjudication

| Gate | Requirement | Result |
|---|---|---|
| A | Nonconstant \(\mathcal A_G(s)\) from fixed \(\mathcal D_G,\mathsf M_E\) | **FAIL — exact fixed-image theorem** |
| B | Not state selection or conditioning | **FAIL for source level sets and pure-fiber slices** |
| C | Not state reweighting or objective change | **FAIL for measure/action constructions** |
| D | Not merely operational reachability | **FAIL for state channels** |
| E | Sourcehood derived from absolute compatibility defect | **FAIL — compatible realizations have zero defect** |
| F | Intrinsic multi-source composition from current primitives | **FAIL — no source representation/action exists** |

Final classification:

\[
\boxed{
\texttt{NONTRIVIAL\_SOURCE\_DEPENDENT\_ADMISSIBILITY\_REQUIRES\_A\_NEW\_KINEMATIC\_PRIMITIVE}
}
\]

---

## 9. Scientific meaning

The completed canary program has located a precise missing object.

The frozen theory already contains:

- global positivity;
- marginal compatibility;
- pure-realization fibers;
- hidden completion geometry;
- source observables and selective/nonselective instruments;
- conditional pruning maps.

It does **not** contain a source representation that acts on the rules of global realizability. Consequently, it cannot derive a source-dependent compatibility arena.

This result prevents a source-conditioned subset, preferred optimizer, probability bias, or local quantum operation from being renamed a pre-pruning gravity law.

It also refines the constructive target. If sourcehood is to emerge from global admissibility, the theory needs an extended compatibility structure in which a defect is nonzero while the enlarged global object remains valid, the defect has a canonical local restriction, and independent defects compose.

---

## 10. Claim boundaries and stopping rule

### Exact theorem

With fixed \(\mathcal D_G\) and fixed \(\mathsf M_E\), the compatibility image is fixed. Predicates, channels, measures, and objectives produce selection, reachability, weighting, or optimization, not a new arena.

### Structural conclusion

A nontrivial pre-pruning source law requires an additional kinematic primitive: a source-indexed feasible set, cone, gluing map, factorization, or extended defect-bearing global object, together with a composition law.

### Not established

- that nature contains such a primitive;
- that any candidate primitive is gravity;
- that a compatibility defect produces mass, attraction, geometry, or Einstein dynamics;
- that pruning or physical time already occurs pre-pruning.

### Stopping rule

Do not run another ensemble on the frozen source constructions. The next legitimate task is **axiom synthesis and uniqueness**, not numerical mining:

\[
\boxed{
\text{Find the minimal covariant, compositional extension in which sourcehood is a global defect.}
}
\]

Any proposed extension must state explicitly which frozen primitive it changes and must recover the source-free architecture at \(s=0\).

END EXACT DOCUMENT

# UQCF-GEM v9.72 — Native Incidence and Composition Gate

## Adjudication

**NATIVE FIXED-GRAPH INCIDENCE IS AVAILABLE. A SOURCE-TO-NEW-INCIDENCE ASSIGNMENT IS NOT ESTABLISHED IN THE INSPECTED DEFINITIONS. ASSOCIATIVE COMBINATION OF SPECIFICATIONS DOES NOT GUARANTEE COMPOSITION OF OVERLAPPING QUANTUM REALIZATIONS.**

This gate resolves the target proposed in v9.71 by separating three operations: repeating an existing obligation, combining disjoint systems, and combining overlapping specifications. None supplies the missing provenance-to-obligation law by itself. The result is an audit and elementary exact clarification, not a gravity discovery or a claim of new quantum-marginal mathematics.

The analysis is entirely atemporal. Partial trace denotes static restriction to a subsystem observable algebra.

## 1. Sources inspected and the bounded finding

Read in full for this gate:

- `UQCF_GEM_v9_50_Source_Dependent_Admissibility_Existence_Gate.md` — frozen tensor factors, density domain and edge marginal map; an additional source representation is required to change admissibility.
- `UQCF_Provenance_Multiplicity_Gate.md` — provenance composition, equivalence and the relation to quantum extensions were not supplied by the inspected native model; additive multiplicity alone does not imply a compatibility coupling.
- `UQCF_Replica_Incidence_Canary.md` — the extra B-role factors, source-token assignment, copy-role identifications and equal AB marginals are explicitly postulated together.

Immediate lineage: v9.69 established fixed-architecture pure-state feasibility sectors conditional on equal-pair obligations; v9.70 did not derive those obligations from common realization, equal locals or covariance; v9.71 classified exact intersection composition for additive scalar sources.

The archive does contain distinct native subsystem factors and graph edges. It would be incorrect to say it contains no incidence. What is absent from these inspected definitions is a canonical assignment from source/provenance to additional independent factors or additional joint obligations, together with the comparison maps needed to compose them. This is a bounded finding about the supplied definitions, not a claim that every historical project artifact has been searched.

## 2. Repeated labels are not independent quantum slots

For two distinct tensor factors B and C, the standard embeddings of their observable algebras into the global algebra commute. They do not become the same embedding when their provenance labels agree.

More precisely, let alpha and beta be injective unital algebra maps from M_d into a common algebra, with commuting images and d > 1. They cannot be equal. If alpha=beta, then for all X,Y,

\[
\alpha([X,Y])=[\alpha(X),\beta(Y)]=0.
\]

Injectivity would imply every pair of matrices in M_d commutes, a contradiction.

Thus literal identification of the observable algebra and independent tensor-factor replication are different constructions. The argument does not prohibit equality of selected state expectations on the two algebras; that is a constraint on the state, as in v9.70. Nor does it prohibit explicit isomorphisms between separate factors. Those isomorphisms do not identify their global embeddings.

## 3. Disjoint composition: existence, without a shared-interface effect

For disjoint systems G and H, the supplied tensor factors give a canonical product state for any specified density operators:

\[
\rho_G\otimes\sigma_H.
\]

It reproduces both component states. It is globally pure when both inputs are pure. Tensor product is associative up to the standard reassociation of factors.

For the full-density domains and a graph union having no cross edges,

\[
\mathcal A_{G\sqcup H}=\mathcal A_G\times\mathcal A_H.
\]

Proof: restricting a global density gives valid component densities, proving one inclusion. Tensoring witnesses for any two component edge data proves the reverse inclusion.

The corresponding equality is not asserted for arbitrary globally pure domains: an entangled pure state across G and H can have mixed component marginals. Product composition of pure witnesses proves existence for the product of the component pure images, not exhaustion of the larger pure image.

Disjoint composition supplies a baseline realization, but does not impose that two partners share identical correlations with one A. Introducing a common interface changes the problem.

## 4. Overlapping composition: an exact obstruction

Given states on AB and AC, agreement on A is necessary for a joint ABC realization. It is not sufficient, even if both specified states are pure.

Take qubits and prescribe

\[
\rho_{AB}=|\Phi^+\rangle\langle\Phi^+|,
\qquad
\sigma_{AC}=|\Phi^+\rangle\langle\Phi^+|.
\]

Both restrictions to A equal I_2/2. Suppose a positive normalized tau_ABC realizes both. Since its AB marginal is pure, positivity forces

\[
\tau_{ABC}=\rho_{AB}\otimes\tau_C.
\]

For completeness, let P be the rank-one support projector of rho_AB and Q=I-P. The positive operator tau has zero expectation on the subspace Q tensor H_C. Positivity then annihilates that subspace, including its cross blocks, so tau is supported on P tensor H_C and factors as stated.

Consequently its AC marginal is (I_2/2) tensor tau_C, which cannot equal a pure Bell projector. Contradiction. Allowing a mixed global completion does not repair the obstruction.

Define the matching-overlap set

\[
\mathcal F=\{(\rho_{AB},\sigma_{AC}):\rho_A=\sigma_A\}.
\]

The actual marginal map from D(ABC) has image strictly inside F. Therefore no total composition map on all of F can preserve both prescribed component states. This impossibility holds before asking whether the map is canonical, covariant, or associative.

The Bell witness is a standard compatibility obstruction used here to fix the precise meaning of composition. It is not a new physical law.

## 5. What remains associative

For three fixed subsystem specifications AB, AC and AD, their simultaneous realization fiber is

\[
\{\tau_{ABCD}:\tau_{AB}=\rho,\ \tau_{AC}=\sigma,\ \tau_{AD}=\upsilon\}.
\]

The order and parenthesization of these equations do not change this set. It may be empty. Thus associative conjunction of specifications is compatible with failure of existence of a joint state.

There is also a precise projection issue. For any map p and subsets C,D of its domain,

\[
p(C\cap D)\subseteq p(C)\cap p(D),
\]

and equality need not hold: separate witnesses for the same visible value need not be a common witness. In the Bell example, take the domain D(ABC), let C prescribe the AB Bell state, D prescribe the AC Bell state, and let p restrict to A. Then p(C)=p(D)={I_2/2}, while C intersection D is empty.

This explains why multiplying source counts in a replica hierarchy is not the same operation as intersecting its projected pair-state sets. The larger incidence object retains simultaneous realization requirements that the separate projections omit. It is a legitimate way to avoid v9.71's exact-intersection premise, but it requires the larger incidence object in the first place.

## 6. Gate table

| Candidate composition | What is supplied | What is not derived |
|---|---|---|
| Repeat one fixed obligation | Idempotent conjunction | Additive magnitude response |
| Combine disjoint systems | Tensor-product realization | Shared-interface obligations or interaction |
| Unite overlapping specifications | Simultaneous marginal equations | Guaranteed joint realization |
| Add replica slots per source token | Conditional shareability hierarchy | Native source-to-slot assignment and equal-pair law |

Graph relabeling does not create a new tensor factor. Existing degree counts distinct native neighbors, but interpreting degree as physical source strength and requiring equal relations to those neighbors remain separate claims. The graph alone does not impose those equalities.

## 7. Decision and handoff boundary

The requested native-incidence derivation is not established. The mathematical controls survive: independent incidences can impose genuinely joint compatibility conditions; their consequences can be nontrivial without fitting a response amplitude. But the source-to-incidence assignment remains physical input.

**Close the repeated search for this assignment inside the inspected fixed architecture.** Do not schedule another native replica simulation or rename the conditional shareability result as source-derived gravity.

Any continuation of this specific mechanism must supply a concrete new axiom or an additional source artifact defining the missing structure. Its review must identify: which incidences are physical; which provenance equivalences preserve them; how sources determine obligations; and whether composition means partial joint realizability or an everywhere-defined operation preserving component states. The latter is already excluded on unrestricted overlapping quantum inputs by section 4.

This gate does not select a replacement axiom. It prevents mathematical associativity at the specification level from being mistaken for a derived physical coupling or a guarantee that realized sources compose.

## 8. Evidence boundary

The algebra-embedding obstruction, disjoint full-density factorization, overlapping Bell obstruction, and projection inclusion have explicit proofs above. No numerical simulation or new numerical verification was performed or needed. The source audit is limited to the named reports. No claim is made that gravity before time is impossible; the specific missing coupling has not been derived.

END EXACT DOCUMENT

# Provenance multiplicity and extendability: theorem gate

Date: 2026-09-05. Unnumbered continuation checkpoint; no missing v9 version is reconstructed or claimed.

## Adjudication

**NATIVE APPLICATION BLOCKED BY UNSPECIFIED COMPOSITION/EQUIVALENCE; ADDITIVE MULTIPLICITY ALONE DOES NOT DERIVE AN EXTENSION LAW.**

No ensemble simulation was performed. This report gives elementary conditional algebraic results and corrects the proposed discriminating test. It is not a claim of new mathematical novelty or a gravitational discovery.

The approved target was to test whether provenance-derived multiplicity survives legitimate equivalence and has a non-inserted effect on joint extendability. The recovered v9 quantum architecture supplies states, marginal maps and compatibility fibers. The inspected sources do not supply a native composable provenance object, its presentation congruence, and a provenance-to-quantum extension relation sufficient to instantiate this target.

This is a bounded retrieval/application gap, not a theorem that no such definitions occur anywhere in the project.

## 1. Source grounding and branch firewall

Sources read in full for this gate:

- UQCF_GEM_v9_50_Source_Dependent_Admissibility_Existence_Gate.md — libfile_dd4cb2b8d6d8819186eebb55fc4ee513.
- UQCF_GEM_v9_43_Separating_Prediction_Report.md — libfile_4984a24daf108191a658c6ae7581f784.
- UQCF_GEM_v7_1_Contextual_Congruence_Recovery_Dagger_Report.md — libfile_e0c00c43e84481918bd4362cf74e300f.

v9.50 explicitly requires a new source representation/action for nonconstant source-dependent admissibility. v9.43 distinguishes rejection of provenance records from exclusion of quantum configurations and observable values. v7.1 supplies a conditional historical contextual-equivalence construction: histories are equivalent if all legitimate left/right contexts return identical native outcomes. It still requires the native outcome family. That historical construction is not automatically an additional primitive of the v9 quantum model.

The Library skill was used to recover the exact sources and retain this gate. No historical scaffold was simulated or substituted for the native model.

## 2. First distinction: which composition?

There are at least three mathematically different operations:

1. Sequential composition of transformations or composable provenance morphisms.
2. Parallel combination of independent source-bearing systems.
3. Union/intersection of compatibility constraints or gluing specifications.

They need not induce the same additive law. A reversibility obstruction for operation composition does not rule out a positive charge additive under tensor product. An idempotence obstruction for constraint conjunction does not show all richer gluing composition is idempotent.

Before applying any theorem below, identify the actual operation, its unit, its domain, and the equivalences imposed on it. Atemporal composition does not require physical chronology.

## 3. Exact descent criterion for multiplicity

Suppose raw provenance words form a monoid P and legitimate presentation equivalence ~ is a composition congruence. Let d:P -> R satisfy d(pq)=d(p)+d(q), d(e)=0.

**Proposition.** There is a well-defined additive D:P/~ -> R with d=D composed with the quotient map if and only if d(p)=d(q) whenever p~q.

Proof: Necessity follows because a function has one value on each equivalence class. Conversely define D([p])=d(p); fiber constancy makes it well-defined, and additivity descends. The same argument applies to composable arrows in a category with matching interfaces.

For a monoid presentation with generators a and relations u~v, assign generator weights w_a. The additive word value is the sum of generator weights with multiplicity. It descends precisely when every defining relation has equal total weight on its two sides. Stability under composition then makes all generated relations preserve that value.

An unweighted event count therefore fails whenever one legitimate event can be subdivided into two equivalent events. Weighted additivity can survive subdivision only if the primitive weights are independently supplied and satisfy the relation; invariance does not select those weights by itself.

### Universal additive object

Define the abelian group U(P) with symbols [p] and relations [pq]=[p]+[q], [e]=0, and [p]=[q] for legitimate equivalences. Every real additive invariant factors uniquely through a group homomorphism U(P)->R.

This is a classification device, not a physical source construction. The allowed nonnegative invariants are the homomorphisms whose values on all provenance classes are nonnegative. A vector-space dimension or a preferred positive ray can only be discussed after these objects and constraints are specified. Even one positive ray leaves normalization unselected unless another premise fixes it.

## 4. Two immediate algebraic obstructions

### Reversible-composition obstruction

Suppose each provenance arrow p has an inverse p^-1 modulo legitimate equivalence, and D takes values in the nonnegative reals. Then

0 = D(e) = D(p^-1 p) = D(p^-1)+D(p).

Both summands are nonnegative, so both are zero. Thus a nonnegative additive invariant vanishes on the reversible sector.

A slightly stronger conditional version needs only a generalized inverse q satisfying p q p ~ p: additivity gives 2D(p)+D(q)=D(p), hence D(p)+D(q)=0; nonnegativity again forces D(p)=D(q)=0.

Signed real invariants are not ruled out on arbitrary groups. Finite-order elements nevertheless have zero real additive value because p^n~e implies nD(p)=0. Do not generalize this finite-order argument to an arbitrary infinite history monoid. No claim is made here that native provenance identifies a reversible loop with the identity merely because its quantum endpoint returns.

### Idempotence obstruction

If p p ~ p, additivity gives 2D(p)=D(p), hence D(p)=0. This holds even for signed real values. Therefore repeat-insensitive constraint conjunction cannot faithfully encode repeat-sensitive real additive strength through the conjunction result alone.

If a source-to-constraint map sends repeated sources to the same constraints, a nonzero source count may still exist upstream. It simply cannot be recovered from that idempotent image. Additional retained multiplicity is then real extra structure, whose action must separately be justified.

These obstructions do not prove that every pre-pruning source is zero. They identify combinations of assumptions that cannot support the proposed positive additive defect.

## 5. Correction to the approved extendability test

The earlier proposed test compared p and q with equal present visible data, different multiplicity, and different allowed extensions. That is insufficient on its own to establish a new source-to-kinematics coupling.

Let B be the frozen quantum configurations, O:B->Y the observation map, and C a fixed space of extension candidates with matching declared interfaces. Let R subset B x C be the frozen quantum compatibility relation. Define

Ext_0(x) = {c : R(x,c)}.

For visible data y, the existential extension set is

Ext_0(y) = union over x with O(x)=y of Ext_0(x).

Two different hidden configurations x and x' can have the same O-value but different Ext_0(x) and Ext_0(x'). That would identify hidden-completion information under the same law R. It does not show R changed.

Consequently, finding such a pair could be useful as a completion witness, but would not meet the stronger mechanism claim in this branch. This distinction is a correction to the proposal, not a newly discovered effect in a native ensemble.

If p and q are equivalent under *all* admissibility-sensitive extension contexts, different extension sets contradict that equivalence by definition. We must distinguish this full contextual equivalence from present-data equality or presentation-only equivalence. The same word “equivalent” cannot serve all three roles.

## 6. Matched conditional extension criterion

Suppose a separately justified relation B_p subset B specifies which quantum configurations realize provenance sector p. Define

Ext_p(y) = {c : there exists x in B_p with O(x)=y and R(x,c)}.

An extension candidate c is absent from Ext_p(y) if and only if every baseline completion x with O(x)=y and R(x,c) lies outside B_p. This is the existential-to-universal projection criterion of v9.43 applied to extensions.

To attribute a conditional extension difference to provenance, the relation B_p must be supplied independently of the desired result. To attribute it specifically to the multiplicity D rather than other provenance information, show that the declared extension prediction factors through D (and the retained interface/data): equal D and matched retained data must give the same prediction on the stated domain.

Even then the result is a conditional compatibility law. Whether the extended provenance theory makes B_p a fundamental kinematic sector or merely a selected subset must be stated explicitly. A difference in conditioned extension sets does not overturn the fixed-image theorem.

## 7. Why additivity alone cannot supply the missing coupling

Take any baseline quantum configuration space B and any provenance monoid P with an additive invariant D. Form the decorated space B x P. Permit extensions solely by the baseline relation R on B, independent of P. All presentation invariance and additivity properties of D remain true; quantum extension possibilities do not depend on it.

This is a logical countermodel, not an adopted UQCF extension. It proves that additivity and well-defined multiplicity alone cannot force a compatibility response. If P and D also have an appropriate covariance action, the product construction inherits it while the quantum extension relation can remain provenance-blind. Extra reduction or gluing axioms would need to be specified and checked separately; they are not presumed satisfied by an unspecified model.

The neutral P sector reproduces the baseline quantum sector. Thus neutral reduction and composable multiplicity by themselves do not remove the decoupled possibility.

## 8. Gate scorecard

| Gate | Outcome |
|---|---|
| Preserve v9.43 and v9.50 boundaries | PASS |
| Recover a native v9 provenance monoid/category and equivalence | NOT ESTABLISHED in inspected sources |
| Give an exact multiplicity-descent criterion | PASS, conditional elementary theorem |
| Exclude positive additive operation counts on reversible quotient sectors | PASS, conditional exact obstruction |
| Exclude additive strength represented only by idempotent conjunction | PASS, conditional exact obstruction |
| Show different hidden extensions imply changed kinematics | FAIL; proposed discriminator corrected |
| Derive a nontrivial native multiplicity-to-extension coupling | NOT REACHED |
| Run a simulation | STOP |

## 9. Next necessary input

The next work is not another numerical observable. It is a precise source specification: provenance objects and interfaces; sequential versus parallel composition; legitimate equivalence; the native additive target; and the relation linking provenance sectors to quantum gluing.

If this already exists in unrecovered v9.51-onward work, its source artifact should control the next gate. Otherwise it must be proposed and labeled as new-axiom synthesis. The first falsifier is whether its quantum projection remains surjective for every sector; if so, no hard-admissibility response has been established. If not, an excluded extension needs a complete baseline-feasibility and sector-infeasibility certificate.

The useful outcome of this gate is a sharper test, not a new gravity claim: intrinsic multiplicity, nonnegative source strength, compositional compatibility and a physical response law are four separate obligations.

END EXACT DOCUMENT

# Replica-incidence canary: explicit coupling and exact falsification

Date: 2026-09-05. Unnumbered new-axiom candidate, following the provenance-multiplicity gate.

## Outcome

**CONDITIONAL MULTIPLICITY-SENSITIVE COMPATIBILITY FOUND; NATIVE PROVENANCE AND PURE-STATE TRANSFER NOT ESTABLISHED.**

One explicit source-to-gluing hypothesis produces a nonconstant compatibility hierarchy and a certified joint-composition obstruction without specifying a force, response amplitude or optimization objective. The consequence is ordinary quantum shareability/monogamy, not a newly discovered quantum law. The hypothesis does not follow from frozen provenance, and its mixed-state construction cannot silently be transferred to a fixed globally pure realization.

No random ensemble, fitted response or physical simulation was run. Small exact rational matrix identities were checked to validate the explicit witness. No v9 version number is assigned because later version artifacts have not been recovered.

## 1. What is postulated

**Replica-incidence hypothesis:** each additional elementary source token requires one additional interchangeable partner slot, and all source–partner pair marginals must agree as marginals of one positive normalized global state.

This is a candidate new law, not a provenance extraction theorem. The following ingredients are being supplied together and must remain visible:

- a distinguished bipartite interface A–B;
- a finite set of source tokens, modulo bijective relabeling;
- one extra B-role factor per token;
- specified identifications of those copies with the B role;
- equality of all A–B-role marginal states;
- global PSD normalization on the enlarged tensor product.

Counting tokens is intrinsic to this stipulated finite-set provenance model. It is not shown intrinsic to the frozen UQCF provenance. Replacing one token by two is not an equivalence in this candidate. If native presentation equivalence permits such a replacement, the candidate's count does not descend and the candidate fails that transfer gate.

The hypothesis changes global degrees of freedom as well as gluing constraints. It is therefore NOT a successful deformation of gluing alone with all original Hilbert factors held fixed. No Hilbert-space transition map is extracted from a density matrix. Copy-role identifications are explicit new data; their introduction is part of the cost of the candidate.

## 2. Source composition and neutral sector

Let source provenance be finite sets R up to bijection, with composition disjoint union. Define n=|R| and k=n+1, where the original partner B_1 is retained even for n=0.

Disjoint union gives n(R disjoint-union S)=n(R)+n(S), with the empty set neutral. Composition is associative and symmetric up to the ordinary relabeling isomorphisms. This is parallel multiplicity, not reversible operation composition, so the preceding positive-additive group obstruction does not apply.

For fixed H_A and H_B define

E_k = {rho_AB : there exists sigma on H_A tensor H_B^tensor-k, sigma >= 0, Tr sigma=1, and sigma_ABj=rho_AB for every j}.

All pair marginal equalities use the declared copy-role identifications. A permutation-invariant extension may be obtained by averaging over partner permutations without changing any pair marginal. This statement concerns mixed-state extensions; averaging is not a method for preserving a pure global state.

At n=0, k=1 and E_1=D(H_A tensor H_B). Thus the neutral sector exactly recovers the two-factor FULL-DENSITY baseline. It does not claim recovery of every frozen pure-state graph model.

For a larger original graph G, a possible explicit mixed-state generalization would require sigma restricted to G to equal X_G and impose equality between its original AB marginal and every extra AB_j marginal. At n=0 this again recovers the original full-density domain and its fixed marginal map. The pure-state obstruction to that generalization is proved below.

## 3. Covariance and reduction

If rho transforms by U_A tensor U_B, transform its extension by U_A tensor U_B^tensor-k. Membership in E_k is unchanged. Permuting replica labels is also immaterial. This is covariance of the declared abstract role construction.

If the replicas are represented as independently coordinatized physical subsystems with maps i_j:H_B->H_Bj, independent local basis transformations must transform i_j as well. Keeping the identifications fixed while rotating replicas independently would not preserve the equality constraints. Therefore unrestricted native local covariance is not certified by merely writing equal marginals; the added role-identification data must be included in the kinematic object.

Tracing out partner slots gives

E_(k+1) subseteq E_k.

All separable pair states pass every level: if rho_AB=sum_t p_t alpha_t tensor beta_t, then sigma=sum_t p_t alpha_t tensor beta_t^tensor-k is an extension. In particular, increasing count need not force any response at all for such states. This is an exact null family.

Partner deletion and local coordinate covariance are established. They are not a proof of every possible native graph-coarsening or subsystem-reduction axiom.

## 4. What associative composition does and does not guarantee

A specification with n tokens has k=n+1 partner slots. Combining n and m tokens keeps one common original B_1 and takes disjoint additional slots, giving k_total=n+m+1. Associativity holds at the incidence-specification level.

For specifications sharing A and B_1, however, separate quantum extensions need not possess a joint extension on the union of slots. Equal overlap marginals are necessary but not sufficient for quantum gluing.

Consequently,

E_(n+m+1) subseteq E_(n+1) intersection E_(m+1),

and the inclusion can be strict. The right side forgets joint realizability on the combined incidence object.

This does not violate associativity. It distinguishes associative combination of constraints from guaranteed existence of a state satisfying the combined constraints. If the intended source-composition axiom instead requires every pair of individually admissible source realizations to compose without obstruction, this candidate fails that stronger axiom.

Entirely disjoint A–B systems can be combined by tensor product of extensions. Their composition is not the shared-interface composition above. No “interaction” between disjoint sources is thereby derived.

## 5. Exact qubit obstruction, derived rather than fitted

Take H_A=H_B=C^2. Choose a singlet projector P_- as a witness on the pair, and let F=Tr(P_- rho_AB). Its choice is a diagnostic, not a preferred basis or singlet law inserted into the candidate. The whole witness family transforms under local unitaries; feasibility itself is basis invariant.

On A B_1 ... B_k define

Q_k = sum_(j=1)^k P_-^(A Bj).

If an equal-marginal extension sigma exists, then Tr(Q_k sigma)=k F.

Writing spin operators S_A=Pauli_A/2 and J_B=sum_j Pauli_Bj/2 gives the operator identity

Q_k = (k/4) I - S_A dot J_B.

For partner total spin j and joint spin j+1/2, the scalar product has eigenvalue j/2. For joint spin j-1/2, when j>=1/2, it has eigenvalue -(j+1)/2. Since j<=k/2, the greatest eigenvalue of Q_k is (k+1)/2, attained in the maximal partner-spin sector with the lower joint spin.

Therefore every k-extendible pair state obeys

F <= (k+1)/(2k).

This is a necessary witness inequality for general pair states; it is not asserted sufficient for arbitrary two-qubit densities. The normalized maximal-eigenspace projector is partner-permutation invariant and rotation invariant, so its pair marginals are equal Werner states saturating the bound. Thus the bound is sharp on that symmetric family.

| Extra tokens n | Partner count k | Maximum singlet overlap |
|---|---|---|
| 0 | 1 | 1 |
| 1 | 2 | 3/4 |
| 2 | 3 | 2/3 |

The decreasing upper bound is a compatibility limit, not a dynamical response or a choice of the state after adding a token.

## 6. Complete finite witness for failure of automatic gluing

Set

rho_* = (3/4) P_- + (1/12)(I-P_-).

It is PSD, normalized, and has F=3/4.

For k=2 write Q=Q_2 and define

sigma_2 = Q^2/3 - Q/6.

The exact identities checked are:

- Q(Q-I/2)(Q-3I/2)=0;
- sigma_2 is real symmetric;
- sigma_2^2=sigma_2/2;
- Tr sigma_2=1;
- Tr_B2 sigma_2=Tr_B1 sigma_2=rho_* under copy identifications.

Real symmetry and the quadratic identity put the eigenvalues of sigma_2 in {0,1/2}; trace one makes it a positive normalized rank-two state. This supplies an explicit extension, not merely a solver claim.

For k=3 the exact identity

Q_3(Q_3-I/2)(Q_3-3I/2)(Q_3-2I)=0

and real symmetry imply Q_3<=2I. Any equal-marginal three-partner extension of rho_* would have Tr(Q_3 sigma)=3*(3/4)=9/4>2, impossible.

Hence rho_* belongs to E_2 but not E_3. Two one-token specifications, each separately feasible for this pair state, have an infeasible two-token joint specification. The singlet-overlap exclusion margin is 1/12.

Verification used Python standard-library Fraction arithmetic and explicit 8x8 and 16x16 matrices, with no floating-point tolerances. It checked the displayed annihilating polynomials, marginal identities and projector normalization. A first optional SymPy attempt was unavailable; the successful verification required no external symbolic package. The proof does not depend on the computational check.

This is a known quantum marginal/shareability phenomenon. It is not a newly discovered source law.

## 7. Pure-state transfer obstruction

The explicit sigma_2 above is mixed, of rank two. Calling it a globally pure native realization would be incorrect.

More generally, suppose the enlarged state sigma_GC is required to preserve the entire original pure state X_G=|psi><psi| as its marginal on G. Then

sigma_GC = |psi><psi| tensor tau_C.

Proof: the marginal has support on a one-dimensional subspace of H_G. Positivity forces sigma_GC to have support in that subspace tensor H_C, which gives the factorization. This holds even when the enlarged state is allowed to be mixed.

In particular, for an additional partner B_j outside G,

sigma_ABj = rho_A tensor tau_Bj.

It cannot equal a correlated original rho_AB. Thus preserving the full original pure realization while requiring a correlated pair relation to reappear with an added partner is impossible already at the first replica.

If the original system is just pure AB, only product pure pair states survive the first extra partner requirement; they survive all further ones by product extension. The graded mixed-state hierarchy does not survive that pure two-factor specialization.

One could instead preserve only selected marginal data, change the original global state, or allow an ancillary purification. Those are distinct enlarged specifications. This report does not silently adopt any of them or claim that they recover the native pure graph theory. Allowing a hidden ancilla solely to rescue a mixed witness would add another degree-of-freedom premise.

## 8. Native provenance and gravity discrimination

The multiplicity effect is not a harmless record label: under the explicit replica rule it removes baseline pair states at higher counts. However, the rule identifying source tokens with equal-marginal replica obligations is POSTULATED. It is not derived from an Omega_ij provenance state, a source observable, or the neutral theory.

The derived inequality is standard quantum monogamy once replica gluing is required. The coefficient and monotonicity in that inequality are not fitted, but the choice of replica gluing rather than another source law is new physical input.

No canonical state selection follows when a baseline state becomes excluded. There is no derived attraction, sign of mechanical response, exterior universal field, spatial range or pruning-to-geometry bridge. Increasing replica count is not a demonstrated native-resolution limit. Separable states remain compatible at every count.

Therefore the proper claim is a concrete example of how an explicit incidence axiom can produce multiplicity-sensitive feasibility—not provenance-derived gravity and not an axiom uniquely selected by covariance and composition.

## 9. Gate adjudication

| Requirement | Result |
|---|---|
| Explicit source object and equivalence | PASS for stipulated finite-token model; not native-derived |
| Additive, relabeling-invariant multiplicity | PASS within that model |
| Neutral recovery | PASS for full-density baseline; not a full native pure-state transfer |
| Covariance | PASS with declared copy-role structure; unqualified native covariance not established |
| Associative specification composition | PASS |
| Guaranteed gluing of individually valid overlapping realizations | FAIL by exact witness |
| Proper nonconstant feasibility hierarchy | PASS in the mixed-state construction |
| Non-inserted quantitative consequence | PASS conditionally: sharp quantum shareability bound |
| Preserve correlated original globally pure realization | FAIL already at first replica |
| Derived native provenance-to-incidence law | NOT ESTABLISHED |
| Distinguish consequence from standard quantum shareability | FAIL as a gravity-specific discriminator |
| Launch native ensemble simulation | STOP |

## 10. Research decision

Retain this construction as a mathematical control and explicit coupling example. Do not promote it to the next native canary ensemble and do not add a relaxation objective to manufacture a response.

The next admissible theoretical target must specify how actual provenance supplies higher incidence without copying an entire correlated relation across new partner factors, and how the zero-source and pure-state domains compare. It must also say whether source composition means associative constraints with potentially empty joint fibers or an everywhere-defined gluing of realized sectors. Those are different axioms; the example separates them exactly.

## Sources and novelty boundary

- Prior checkpoint: UQCF_Provenance_Multiplicity_Gate.md, source identifier libfile_7ada8bf4b3f88191845ed461fb6c04c2.
- Frozen architecture: UQCF_GEM_v9_50_Source_Dependent_Admissibility_Existence_Gate.md, source identifier libfile_dd4cb2b8d6d8819186eebb55fc4ee513; read in full in the preceding gate, setting reconfirmed here.
- Jakab, Solymos and Zimboras, [Extendibility of Werner States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2208.13743): established literature on one-/two-sided equal-marginal extension, its symmetry reductions and exact compatibility bounds. This report derives its small-qubit witness explicitly; it does not claim novelty for shareability or the spin-spectrum method.
- Chen, Ji, Kribs, Lutkenhaus and Zeng, [Symmetric Extension of Two-Qubit States](https://arxiv.org/abs/1310.3530): an independent established characterization of the two-qubit symmetric-extension problem. No new general two-qubit criterion is claimed here.

The Library skill was used to retain the prior gate and save this result. Existing research artifacts were not modified.

# UQCF-GEM v7.16 — Retained Atlas Groupoid Reconciliation

## Correction to v7.15

The v7.15 stop was correct for **ordered update / repair histories**, but it was
too broad if read as saying that no reversible morphism-level object existed
anywhere in the frozen Retained Bridge stack.

An earlier frozen atlas construction already defines

\[
T_{ij}=B_j^{-1}B_i.
\]

Therefore

\[
T_{ji}T_{ij}=I,
\]

and

\[
T_{jk}T_{ij}=T_{ik}.
\]

For triples,

\[
T_{ki}T_{jk}T_{ij}=I.
\]

The executable atlas uses every ordered pair of distinct charts. After adding the
canonical identities \(T_{ii}=I\), this is exactly the pair groupoid on the chart
set.

For five charts,

\[
\mathbb C[\mathrm{Pair}(5)]\cong M_5(\mathbb C),
\]

which is noncommutative.

Thus a finite reversible noncommutative algebraic sector was already present at
the retained-atlas transition level.

## Why this does not yet close the native bridge

The V1689 retained connection \(\Gamma_R\) is stricter than an invertible matrix
atlas. A full admissible transport must preserve:

- branch generators,
- the native retained recombination product,
- the L3 associator obstruction,
- the L4 residual,
- source/provenance structure,
- retained-order direction.

The standalone global-atlas proof establishes matrix inverse consistency,
cocycle closure, loop closure, and retained chart-label admissibility. It does
not by itself prove that both directions of a nonidentity edge satisfy the full
generated-algebra faithfulness conditions.

So the correct distinction is

\[
\boxed{
\text{atlas transition groupoid: closed}
}
\]

while

\[
\boxed{
\text{full native }\Gamma_R\text{ generated-algebra groupoid: open}.
}
\]

## Consequence

The noncommutative branch should not be abandoned. It should move to one exact
audit:

> Find a paired \(p\to q\) and \(q\to p\) full-stack \(\Gamma_R\) edge and verify
> both the inverse equations and every frozen generated-algebra faithfulness
> condition.

A pass would provide the first fully native nontrivial reversible retained
algebra sector without deriving a dagger from pruning recovery.

# UQCF-GEM v7.2 — Fully Recoverable Groupoid Core and Positivity

## Theorem

Let \(\mathcal G\) be the total uniquely recoverable sector of retained histories. If every morphism in this sector has a genuine inverse, \(\mathcal G\) is a groupoid.

Linearize it over \(\mathbb C\). For

\[
a=\sum_{g\in\mathcal G} c_g g,
\qquad
a^\dagger=\sum_g \overline{c_g}\,g^{-1},
\]

define, for each object \(u\),

\[
\omega_u(a)=\text{coefficient of }1_u.
\]

Then

\[
\omega_u(a^\dagger a)
=
\sum_{g:s(g)=u}|c_g|^2\ge 0.
\]

Hence \(\omega_u\) is a normalized positive linear functional.

This is important because positivity is obtained from the retained recoverable algebraic structure; a Hilbert space does not have to be postulated first. GNS can be applied afterward.

## Boundary

This directly closes A4 only on the fully recoverable/groupoid core. The full pruning structure can be irreversible and need not be a groupoid. The next unresolved step is whether native pruning itself becomes a conditional expectation onto a retained subalgebra.

END EXACT DOCUMENT

UTF8-BYTES 3429
# UQCF-GEM v7.23 — Quotient Correspondence to UCP Classification

## Main result

Let

\[
\pi:X\to Y
\]

be a finite retained quotient and let

\[
F_y=\pi^{-1}(y),\qquad n_y=|F_y|.
\]

Take the reversible pair-groupoid algebras

\[
\mathcal A_X=M_{|X|}(\mathbb C),
\qquad
\mathcal A_Y=M_{|Y|}(\mathbb C).
\]

Require a coarse-to-fine observable map

\[
\Phi:\mathcal A_Y\to\mathcal A_X
\]

to be UCP, to reproduce the canonical classical ledger pullback

\[
\Phi(E_{yy})=P_y=\sum_{x\in F_y}E_{xx},
\]

and to be invariant under permutations within every fine pruning fiber.

Define

\[
v_y=\frac1{\sqrt{n_y}}\sum_{x\in F_y}|x\rangle,
\qquad
V|y\rangle=v_y,
\]

and

\[
Q_y=P_y-|v_y\rangle\langle v_y|.
\]

Then every such map is

\[
\boxed{
\Phi_C(A)
=
V(C\circ A)V^\dagger
+
\sum_y A_{yy}Q_y,
}
\]

where

\[
\boxed{
C\succeq0,\qquad C_{yy}=1.
}
\]

Conversely every correlation matrix \(C\) defines such a UCP map.

## Consequence 1 — unequal fibers are allowed

The \(*\)-homomorphism obstruction from v7.21 was real, but it was specifically
an obstruction to multiplicative embedding.

It is not an obstruction to UCP coarse-graining.

The executable audit uses pruning fibers of cardinalities

\[
1,\quad2,\quad3
\]

and verifies UCP maps for all three tested correlation kernels.

Thus

\[
\boxed{
\text{unequal multiplicity obstructs }*\text{-homomorphism, not UCP}.
}
\]

## Consequence 2 — the channel is not unique

Two extreme admissible choices are:

\[
C=I,
\]

which removes all coarse off-diagonal coherence, and

\[
C_{yz}=1,
\]

which preserves the normalized uniform coherence mode between every pair of
pruning fibers.

Both are UCP.
Both obey the same fiber symmetry.
Both reproduce exactly the same classical retained-observable pullback.

More generally, every

\[
C\succeq0,\qquad \operatorname{diag}C=1
\]

is allowed.

Therefore

\[
\boxed{
R_\pi+\text{counting symmetry}
\not\Rightarrow
\text{unique noncommutative channel}.
}
\]

## Exact missing datum

The remaining freedom is a positive-definite cross-fiber coherence kernel

\[
C(y,z).
\]

Equivalently,

\[
C_{yz}=\langle\xi_y,\xi_z\rangle
\]

for unit vectors attached to the correspondence.

This is the finite manifestation of the standard correspondence/GNS picture:
a CP map is represented by a correspondence together with distinguished vector
data. The bare correspondence does not determine those matrix coefficients.

## Connection to frozen UQCF structure

The retained-pair signature already contains

\[
G_{\rm pair-context}[p],
\]

described as a signed pair-Gram context.

That is now the obvious native candidate for the missing \(C\).

But its frozen claim level remains a diagnostic. The current project files do
not yet show that these pair contexts assemble into one global coarse-class
matrix with

\[
G\succeq0,\qquad G_{yy}=1,
\]

nor that it is stable under quotient aggregation and retained-order advancement.

So it must not yet be identified with the channel kernel.

## Status

The finite mathematical existence problem is now closed:

\[
\boxed{
\text{finite retained quotient}
+
\text{classical compatibility}
+
\text{fiber symmetry}
\Rightarrow
\text{a family of UCP noncommutative lifts}.
}
\]

The model-specific uniqueness problem remains:

\[
\boxed{
\text{frozen UQCF retained structure}
\overset{?}{\Longrightarrow}
C.
}
\]

The next immediate-stop audit is therefore the native signed pair-Gram context.

END EXACT DOCUMENT

# UQCF-GEM v7.3 — Pruning to Conditional Expectation

## T1 — Canonical unit-ledger expectation

Let \(\mathcal G\) be a finite discrete recoverable groupoid and let
\(\mathbb C[\mathcal G]\) be its complex groupoid \(*\)-algebra. Define

\[
E_0\!\left(\sum_g c_g g\right)
=
\sum_{u\in\mathrm{Obj}(\mathcal G)} c_{1_u}1_u.
\]

Then \(E_0\) fixes the unit/object algebra, is idempotent and bimodular over that algebra, and

\[
E_0(a^\dagger a)_u
=
\sum_{g:s(g)=u}|c_g|^2\ge 0.
\]

Thus the recoverable history algebra canonically carries a conditional expectation onto the identity ledger.

## T2 — Retained subgroupoid restriction

If a pruning operation literally retains a wide, dagger-closed, composition-closed subgroupoid \(\mathcal H\subseteq\mathcal G\), the coefficient restriction

\[
E_{\mathcal H}\left(\sum_g c_g g\right)=\sum_{h\in\mathcal H}c_h h
\]

is the natural conditional-expectation candidate. The supplied finite discrete audit verifies positivity and the bimodule identity on a nontrivial finite-group control.

This is a conditional result: the Retained Bridge's actual pruning must first be proved to have this structural form.

## T3 — Many-to-one merge no-go

For the classical algebra \(\mathbb C^2\), merging two fine states into one retained class does not determine an expectation. For every \(w\in[0,1]\),

\[
E_w(x,y)=\big(wx+(1-w)y\big)(1,1)
\]

is a unital positive idempotent projection onto constants.

Therefore the coarse merge relation alone cannot select a unique conditional expectation. A native state, weighting rule, or symmetry principle is needed.

## Status

A5 is closed for the canonical projection from recoverable histories to the identity ledger, but not for arbitrary irreversible pruning. The next target must identify the exact algebraic type of the Retained Bridge pruning operator before importing relative-entropy or modular machinery.

END EXACT DOCUMENT

# V1689 source recovery: event-action assignments still need a native owner

Date: 2026-11-03 America/Phoenix. Author: GPT Work.
Research head: `1a18308a026c06882b428fbe201b4634c34dfd8f`.
Inspected agent thread: `b58ca2e4f944a8210b72199fb43f1e6279256a10`, through T-307; PR135 comment `6608371404`; T-308 records this recovery in progress.

**Outcome: original V1689 protocols and emitted data recovered; native receiver delivery remains NOT_DERIVED.** The substantive limit is stated by the historical source itself: the common generator was constructed as a structural scaffold, not selected from first principles. The recovered event schema does not supply the event-to-receiver ownership relation. This is a source investigation, not a new theorem or scientific reproduction.

## What the recovery resolves

T-299-dot/T-300 left the original V1689 dependency unresolved. Eight original packages are now preserved byte-for-byte as downloaded, with all 59 members extracted. They cover V1689.0, .3, .5, .9, .10, .14, .15 and .16. This recovers the named source family and its event-action/generator records, rather than substituting V1688.21 or V1698.

The packages contain protocols, reports, CSV tables and JSON manifests. **They contain no generating program or notebook.** T-308's proposed code inspection could therefore not be completed from these packages. A title search returned 70 V1689 items (ending with no continuation cursor); an additional content search returned the protocols/reports, not generating code. These are bounded retrieval facts, not proof that an implementation does not exist elsewhere. No exact implementation alias from V1688.21 to V1689 is established.

[SOURCE_PROVENANCE.json](SOURCE_PROVENANCE.json) binds the resolved original file identities and downloaded bytes. [INSPECTION.json](INSPECTION.json) lists every member, hash, length, CRC, table schema and row count. The included [inspection script](inspect_archives.py) reproduces this archival inspection without importing or running a historical scientific module.

## Strongest positive candidate and admission test

The concrete object is an event-associated algebra action, not another generic vector transport:

\[
A_e:G_p\longrightarrow G'_p,\qquad \rho_e=dA_e|_{G_p}.
\]

V1689.9 explicitly names branch birth/prune, source injection/removal and dependency actions; V1689.14/.15 uses one generator upstream of both transport and event representation:

\[
T_{pq}=\operatorname{Exp}_R(A_{pq}),\qquad
\rho_e=P_e A_{pq}P_e.
\]

This could matter to the current target if the event itself fixed the affected subspace and made it the same retained receiver's available output.

1. **Missing premise attacked:** native event to receiver-owned affected subspace, with a forced effect after the event.
2. **Inherited candidate:** the original provenance event action and its common-generator relation, named above and sourced below.
3. **Adversary:** any realization implementing the justified native update while leaving the designated receiver blind. W_R/W_E remain the benchmark after a correspondence is earned.
4. **Desired consequence:** a fixed receiver-owned observable whose contrast follows from this event action across the justified realization class.
5. **Falsifier at this stage:** the action/projector/generator or its receiver association is prescribed as input; the archived evidence only serializes that assignment or validates its schema.

It does not select them from native ownership. |
| [V1689.15](extracted/V1689_15_common_generator_structural_emitter_package/V1689_15_COMMON_GENERATOR_STRUCTURAL_EMITTER_REPORT.md), generators/event tables and run manifest | `A_source=generated_algebra_difference_common_generator`; `T_source=Exp_R(A_pq)`; event actions name A_edge_id and contain the supplied projector matrix. | The declared common dependency is serialized. No generating code was recovered to independently reconstruct the reported production. |

Its proposed later variational principle is a proposal, not an inherited receiver-delivery law. |

The historical formulas and emitted objects are **INHERITED SOURCE EVIDENCE in their declared simulation/protocol scope**. Their generator selection, affected-subspace assignment, actual-event correspondence and receiver ownership remain **SUPPLIED** for the present physical claim. Actual atemporal event occurrence stays **ACCEPTED INPUT**.

## Literal event coverage and output typing

The V1689.5 and .10 provenance tables have 40 rows each; .15 has 80 across its two pipelines. Their event-type sets are exactly:

`source_injection`, `dependency_addition`, `repair_event`, `order_boundary_crossing`, `provenance_relabel`.

V1689.10/.15 action tables have the same respective 40/80-row event coverage. None of these emitted tables contains `branch_prune` or `source_removal`. The V1689.9 *protocol* table does list the combined categories `branch_birth/prune` and `source_injection/removal`. A requested event class and an emitted instance are different evidence. This coverage finding does not deny that pruning occurs or prove that no other package implements it.

The output records contain a node label, affected_subspace_id, numerical rho_e and, in .15, event_projector_serialized/A_edge_id. They do not include a receiver's complete initial state, its post-event state, an available observable, or an identification of those matrices with the P1 owner's output. The reports do not supply that identification elsewhere in the inspected text. Calling a serialized affected subspace “owned” would insert the relation being sought.

The .15 manifest reports a compatibility residual near 0.796 and cosine near -0.213; .16 interprets compatibility as not established. These are **historical reported values**, not newly recomputed results. Neither their failure nor the source's rhetoric proves that a particular variational principle is necessary for all native models.

## Consequence for W_R/W_E and the next work

There is still no typed joint constraint K linking this historical algebra to the benchmark receiver. No W_R-pass/W_E-fail verdict is justified from these tables. A common generator ties two specified mathematical constructions together; it does not by itself make either construction the receiver's available record.

T-301 through T-307's corrections remain preserved in the thread. More claims about source-only dependence, split dependence or unitary invariance are not needed to resolve this packet. The source itself locates the unearned selections. Do not reopen thermal tests, tune the historical generator, or follow its proposed variational branch merely because the old report calls it “next.”

Re-entry on this lead requires an existing native operation deriving the event's affected subspace and its receiver-owned consequence, with source provenance sufficient to inspect that derivation. Recovering the absent implementation could clarify how the historical assignments were made; the reported schema and unresolved generator do not yet make that recovery a physical advance. A genuinely different native mechanism remains eligible.

Every extracted member is retained without editing, and all eight complete text reads match their corresponding ZIP member bytes (with the text extractor's terminal-LF convention recorded in REPORT_READBACK.json). No member is an implementation source file or notebook under the listed inspected extensions.

Publication readback is recorded in the accompanying PR/thread entry after the commit exists. Existing groupoid reports and prior entries remain unchanged; this is an additive source packet.

# V1689.0 — Retained Connection Object Protocol

**Verdict:** `DERIVATION_PROTOCOL_FROZEN_NO_REGRESSION_TARGET`

## Core reset

The next branch does not ask:

```text
Can a cycle-flow predictor fit a target?
```

It asks:

```text
What object does Γ_R naturally define,
what identity must that object satisfy,
and what observable follows before regression?
```

The missing bridge is not a better scalar holonomy. It is the correct retained curvature object of a generated-algebra connection, plus a source-balanced compatibility identity.

---

## 1. Local generated retained algebra

At each retained sector `p`, define:

```text
G_p = ⟨B_p, O3_p, H4⊥_p, S_p, Π_p, ⊕_p⟩
```

where:

```text
B_p      = retained branch generators
O3_p     = third-order associator obstruction
H4⊥_p    = fourth-order residual outside span(B_p, O3_p)
S_p      = source/access/divergence state
Π_p      = provenance/pruning-order ledger
⊕_p      = native retained recombination product
```

The local object is therefore not a coordinate patch and not a vector space alone. It is a generated retained recombination algebra with provenance order.

The product may be represented abstractly as:

```text
x ⊕_p y
```

or, in one concrete native-kernel form:

```text
x ⊕ y = x + y + γ_xy K(x,y)
K(x,y) = roll(x) ⊙ y − x ⊙ roll(y)
```

The associator is:

```text
A3_p(x,y,z)
=
(x ⊕_p y) ⊕_p z − x ⊕_p (y ⊕_p z)
```

The L4 object is the retained four-way residual component not contained in the span of branches and L3 associators:

```text
H4⊥_p ∉ span(B_p, O3_p)
```

---

## 2. Admissible retained transport

A retained connection edge is:

```text
T_pq : G_p → G_q
```

It is admissible only if it is generated-algebra faithful.

Required conditions:

```text
T_pq(B_p) ≈ B_q
```

```text
T_pq(x ⊕_p y) ≈ T_pq(x) ⊕_q T_pq(y)
```

```text
T_pq(O3_p) ≈ O3_q
```

```text
T_pq(H4⊥_p) ≈ H4⊥_q
```

```text
T_pq(Π_p) respects pruning-order dependencies in Π_q
```

Equivalently:

```text
A_Γ =
A_branch
∧ A_product
∧ A_O3
∧ A_H4
∧ A_source/provenance
∧ A_order-arrow
```

A branch-only map is not a retained connection if it destroys the product, associator, H4 residual, or provenance order.

This permanently downgrades:

```text
Kabsch / Procrustes / Gramian alignment
```

to diagnostics only. They cannot drive Γ_R.

---

## 3. Curvature object

For a loop:

```text
C = (p0 → p1 → ... → pn → p0)
```

define loop transport:

```text
Ω_C,p0 =
T_pn,p0 ∘ ... ∘ T_p1,p2 ∘ T_p0,p1
```

Then retained curvature is:

```text
R_Γ(C)_p0 = Ω_C,p0 − Id_Gp0
```

This is not primarily a scalar. It is a basepoint-normalized endomorphism defect:

```text
R_Γ(C)_p0 ∈ End(G_p0)
```

Because generated-algebra frames may change, the invariant object is the admissible conjugacy class:

```text
[R_Γ(C)_p0] ∈ End_alg(G_p0) / Aut_alg(G_p0)
```

where `Aut_alg(G_p0)` means admissible automorphisms preserving the generated retained algebra, not arbitrary vector-space rotations.

Scalar holonomy is therefore only a diagnostic projection of the richer object.

---

## 4. Covariant comparison

Curvatures at neighboring loops live in different endomorphism spaces:

```text
R_i ∈ End(G_i)
R_j ∈ End(G_j)
```

So raw subtraction is invalid.

Define a retained comparison map:

```text
Comp_{j→i}(R_j)
```

If local transport is invertible enough:

```text
Comp_{j→i}(R_j) = T_ji R_j T_ij
```

If transport is directed/non-invertible, use an admissible directed comparison:

```text
Comp_{j→i}(R_j)
=
Π_i ∘ T_{j→i} ∘ R_j ∘ T_{i→j}^{admissible}
```

Then the discrete retained covariant derivative is:

```text
(D_Γ R_Γ)_{ij}
=
Comp_{j→i}(R_j) − R_i
```

---

## 5. Source-balanced compatibility

The source-free identity is not primary.

A retained recombination system has provenance/source/pruning-order current. Therefore the first candidate identity is:

```text
D_Γ R_Γ = J_P
```

where:

```text
J_P = provenance/source/pruning-order current
```

Define the residual:

```text
E_Γ = D_Γ R_Γ − J_P
```

The compatibility test is:

```text
E_Γ ≈ 0
```

The source-free case is only a special case:

```text
if J_P = 0, then D_Γ R_Γ = 0
```

This is the correct physics analogue: curvature balance is source-balanced before it is source-free.

---

## 6. Proof obligation

### Proposition

If `T_pq` is not generated-algebra faithful, then `R_Γ(C)_p` is not an invariant retained curvature object.

### Proof sketch

Let:

```text
G_p = (V_p, ⊕_p)
G_q = (V_q, ⊕_q)
```

Suppose `T_pq : V_p → V_q` preserves branch vectors but not the product. Then there exist `x,y ∈ G_p` such that:

```text
T_pq(x ⊕_p y)
≠
T_pq(x) ⊕_q T_pq(y)
```

Define the product transport defect:

```text
δ_pq(x,y)
=
T_pq(x ⊕_p y)
−
[T_pq(x) ⊕_q T_pq(y)]
```

If `δ_pq ≠ 0`, then associator transport generically fails:

```text
T_pq A3_p(x,y,z)
≠
A3_q(T_pq x, T_pq y, T_pq z)
```

Because `H4⊥` is defined relative to lower branch/O3 span, H4 transport is also contaminated.

Therefore loop transport:

```text
Ω_C − Id
```

contains a mixture of:

```text
true retained curvature
+ product transport error
+ associator mismatch
+ H4 frame artifact
```

So it is not an invariant curvature object of the retained recombination system.

Thus any compatibility test using non-generated-algebra-faithful transport is mathematically premature.

QED.

---

## 7. Killer null suite

The primary falsifier is:

```text
N_alg_shuffle
```

It preserves surface statistics:

```text
source/access/divergence marginals
O3 norm distribution
H4 norm distribution
cycle length distribution
local graph degree distribution
cycle defect histogram
```

but breaks:

```text
product alignment
O3 transport alignment
H4⊥ transport alignment
provenance/pruning-order alignment
```

Expected result:

```text
valid Γ_R:
  ||D_Γ R_Γ − J_P|| small / structured / convergent

N_alg_shuffle:
  same surface statistics
  same apparent cycle magnitudes
  but ||D_Γ R_Γ − J_P|| fails
```

If `N_alg_shuffle` passes, the identity is not a retained connection law.

---

## 8. Frozen governance

```text
dashboard metrics = visualization only
generic scalar holonomy = diagnostic only
toy weak_target = banned as law evidence
construction recovery ≠ law discovery
target/predictor provenance separation mandatory
generated-algebra faithfulness mandatory
source-balanced closure before source-free closure
no regression rescue without a first-principles identity
```

---

## 9. Next execution conditions

Python is not admissible until an emitter can produce:

```text
G_p generated algebra objects
T_pq retained transports
product faithfulness defects
O3 transport faithfulness defects
H4⊥ transport faithfulness defects
```

EXACT DOCUMENT ResearchHistory/UQCF-GEM/research/physical-bridge/groupoid_identity_ownership/v1689_recovery/extracted/V1689_14_common_infinitesimal_generator_protocol_package/V1689_14_COMMON_INFINITESIMAL_GENERATOR_PROTOCOL.md
