# V1689.14 — Common Infinitesimal Generator Protocol

**Verdict:** `COMMON_GENERATOR_PROTOCOL_FROZEN_NO_EXECUTION`

## Why this is needed

V1689.13 showed:

```text
ρ_P is structurally clean.
ρ_P is normalized.
But ρ_P is not oriented with D_ΓR_Γ.
```

The valid pipeline had:

```text
mean cosine(D_ΓR_Γ, ρ_P(J_P)) ≈ 0.144
J_perp_fraction ≈ 0.989
```

So the issue is no longer target circularity, schema, null placement, or scale.

The issue is orientation.

The event-action current was built from standalone projectors. Those projectors are admissible ledger actions, but they are not induced by the same connection action that produces `T_pq`.

That means the next required object is:

```text
A_pq
```

a common infinitesimal generated-algebra generator.

---

## 1. Central object

Define:

```text
A_pq : G_p → G_q
```

as the infinitesimal retained connection generator.

It must induce transport:

```text
T_pq ≈ Exp_R(A_pq)
```

where `Exp_R` is a retained/directed exponential or finite update map. It does not have to be reversible.

It must also induce event-current representation:

```text
ρ_e = event component of A_pq
```

So:

```text
T_pq
R_Γ
ρ_P(J_P)
```

must all live in the same generator algebra.

---

## 2. What `A_pq` acts on

Since:

```text
G_p = ⟨B_p, O3_p, H4⊥_p, S_p, Π_p, ⊕_p⟩
```

the generator decomposes as:

```text
A_pq =
A_B
+
A_⊕
+
A_O3
+
A_H4
+
A_S
+
A_Π
```

where:

```text
A_B     = branch generator action
A_⊕     = native product derivation action
A_O3    = L3 associator action
A_H4    = L4 residual action
A_S     = source/access/divergence action
A_Π     = provenance/pruning-order incidence action
```

---

## 3. Product derivation condition

A retained connection generator must be approximately derivation-like:

```text
A(x ⊕ y)
≈
A(x) ⊕ y
+
x ⊕ A(y)
+
source/provenance correction
```

The correction term is not arbitrary. It is the generator-side expression of provenance/source current.

This is the first place where the compatibility identity enters naturally.

---

## 4. Associator and H4 covariance

The generator must also satisfy:

```text
A(A3_p)
≈
induced change in O3 across p → q
```

and:

```text
A(H4⊥_p)
≈
induced change in H4⊥ across p → q
```

If this fails, then the transport may move vectors but not the generated algebra.

---

## 5. Curvature from generator composition

For a loop:

```text
C = (p0 → p1 → ... → pn → p0)
```

with generators:

```text
A_01, A_12, ..., A_n0
```

the loop transport is:

```text
Ω_C = Exp_R(A_n0) ... Exp_R(A_01)
```

For small generators:

```text
R_Γ(C)
≈
Σ_edges A_e
+
Σ_{e<f} [A_e, A_f]
+
higher retained terms
```

So curvature arises from:

```text
noncommuting generated-algebra update actions around a retained loop
```

not from scalar holonomy.

Important:

```text
Do not require A_qp = −A_pq.
```

The retained system is pruning-order directed, so reversibility is not assumed.

---

## 6. `ρ_P` from the same generator

For an event:

```text
e ∈ Events(B_ij)
```

define an event projector or incidence selector:

```text
P_event(e)
```

But now the event action is not the projector itself.

It is the projection of the connection generator:

```text
ρ_e
=
P_event(e) A_pq P_event(e)
```

or the signed incidence component of `A_pq` selected by the event.

Then:

```text
ρ_P(J_P,ij)
=
Σ_e σ(e) |m_e| ρ_e / (M_B · G_B)
```

This fixes the V1689.13 issue:

```text
ρ_e orientation now comes from A_pq,
not from standalone projectors.
```

---

## 7. Source-balanced identity

The identity remains:

```text
D_Γ R_Γ = ρ_P(J_P)
```

with residual:

```text
E_Γ = D_Γ R_Γ − ρ_P(J_P)
```

But now both sides are generated by the same infinitesimal connection algebra.

That is the missing bridge.

---

## 8. Required new table: `generators.csv`

Future execution must emit:

```text
pipeline_id
edge_id
from_node
to_node
A_pq_serialized
A_source
A_B_norm
A_product_norm
A_O3_norm
A_H4_norm
A_S_norm
A_Pi_norm
A_order_directed
A_uses_forbidden_fields
```

And `event_actions.csv` must add:

```text
A_edge_id
rho_e_from_A_pq
event_projector_serialized
rho_orientation_source
```

Allowed:

```text
rho_orientation_source = A_pq_component
```

Forbidden:

```text
rho_orientation_source = standalone_projector_only
rho_orientation_source = fitted_to_E
rho_orientation_source = weak_target
```

---

## 9. Generator-level killer nulls

### `N_generator_shuffle`

Preserve:

```text
A_pq norms
transport scalar statistics
cycle topology
source/access/divergence marginals
O3/H4 norms
```

Break:

```text
generator component orientation across edges
```

Expected:

```text
faithfulness or E_Γ fails
```

### `N_event_projection_shuffle`

Preserve events and generators, but attach events to wrong generator components.

Expected:

```text
ρ_P loses orientation with D_ΓR_Γ
```

### `N_commutator_flatten`

Preserve edge generator norms but force:

```text
[A_e, A_f] ≈ 0
```

Expected:

```text
curvature changes/fails while scalar magnitudes may remain similar
```

### `N_reverse_generator`

Reverse directed generator orientation.

Expected:

```text
order/provenance compatibility fails
```

---

## 10. Nonclaims

Do not claim compatibility if:

```text
ρ_e is built from standalone projectors
A_pq is computed from E_Γ
T_pq and ρ_P use different generator spaces
N_generator_shuffle passes
source-free closure is tested before source-balanced closure
```

---

## Final statement

V1689.14 freezes the next mathematical correction:

```text
T_pq and ρ_P must be derived from the same infinitesimal retained connection generator A_pq.
```

The branch has now moved from:

```text
curvature object
```

to:

```text
connection generator
```

which is the correct layer beneath the compatibility identity.

END EXACT DOCUMENT

# V1689.16 — Common-Generator Sub-Branch Postmortem

**Verdict:** `STRUCTURE_CORRECT_COMPATIBILITY_NOT_ESTABLISHED`

## Bottom line

V1689 succeeded structurally but did not establish compatibility.

The branch correctly moved from:

```text
regression target fitting
```

to:

```text
retained connection identity testing
```

But the clean-room generator still did not satisfy the source-balanced compatibility identity.

## What worked

```text
G_p = generated retained recombination algebra
T_pq = generated-algebra transport
R_Γ = basepoint-normalized endomorphism defect
D_ΓR_Γ = covariant curvature comparison
J_P = provenance/source/pruning-order current
ρ_P = event-action representation
A_pq = common generator upstream of both T_pq and ρ_e
```

This is the correct object stack.

## What failed

V1689.15 emitted:

```text
A_pq first
T_pq = Exp_R(A_pq)
ρ_e = P_event A_pq P_event
```

but the result was still:

```text
mean cosine(D_ΓR_Γ, ρ_P(J_P)) ≈ -0.213
mean E_relative_norm ≈ 0.796
```

So compatibility was not established.

## Root cause

The clean-room `A_pq` was structurally valid but not first-principles selected.

It was built from local differences:

```text
ΔB, ΔS, ΔO3, ΔH4, ΔΠ, Δproduct
```

That is enough to emit a schema, but not enough to produce a lawful connection.

The missing object is:

```text
the selection principle for A_pq
```

A retained connection is not just any algebra-faithful map. It must be the native admissible update selected by the retained recombination principle.

## Mathematical gap

The next question is:

```text
What variational or admissibility principle selects A_pq?
```

Candidate:

```text
A_pq minimizes generated-algebra faithfulness action
subject to provenance/source/order current constraints.
```

A first candidate action is:

```text
I_Γ[A]
=
||δ_⊕||²
+
||δ_O3||²
+
||δ_H4||²
+
||δ_S||²
+
||δ_Π||²
+
λ ||source/order constraint||²
```

The goal is not to fit a target.

The goal is to derive whether stationarity gives:

```text
D_Γ R_Γ = ρ_P(J_P)
```

as the compatibility identity.

## What to keep

```text
G_p generated algebra definition
A_Γ faithfulness gates
R_Γ endomorphism defect
D_ΓR_Γ covariant comparison
J_P ledger current
ρ_P event-action representation
boundary mass/algebra scale normalization
upstream full-pipeline nulls
A_pq as common generator
```

## What to downgrade

```text
clean-room A_pq = scaffold only
standalone event projectors = diagnostic only
schema success = methodological success only
```

## Frozen stop rule

Do not continue modifying clean-room `A_pq` heuristically.

No new generator term is admissible unless it is derived from the variational/admissibility principle.

## Next branch

```text
V1690 — Retained Connection Variational Principle
```

The task is not another emitter tweak.

The task is:

```text
derive A_pq from first principles
```

Required deliverables:

```text
1. define admissibility action I_Γ[A]
2. define source/provenance/order constraints
3. derive stationarity condition
4. show whether D_ΓR_Γ = ρ_P(J_P) follows
5. only then run a new emitter
```

## Safe claim

V1689 established the correct retained connection object stack and showed that compatibility requires a principled generator `A_pq`, not target fitting or arbitrary clean-room transport construction.

END EXACT DOCUMENT

# V1689.3 — Provenance / Source Current `J_P` Protocol

**Verdict:** `PROVENANCE_SOURCE_CURRENT_PROTOCOL_FROZEN_NO_EXECUTION`

V1689.2 defined:

```text
(D_Γ R_Γ)_{ij}
```

as the retained covariant comparison of curvature objects across neighboring retained loops/cells.

V1689.3 defines the right-hand side:

```text
J_P,ij
```

the provenance/source/pruning-order current across the same boundary.

---

## 1. Core rule

`J_P` is not a fitted residual.

It is not:

```text
weak_target
scalar holonomy
post-hoc correction term
E_Γ after the fact
```

It is a ledger-derived current emitted independently from curvature.

The identity is:

```text
D_Γ R_Γ = J_P
```

with residual:

```text
E_Γ = D_Γ R_Γ − J_P
```

The source-free identity:

```text
D_Γ R_Γ = 0
```

is only the special case:

```text
J_P = 0
```

---

## 2. Same-boundary rule

Let:

```text
B_ij
```

be the retained comparison boundary used to compute:

```text
(D_Γ R_Γ)_{ij}
```

Then `J_P,ij` must be computed over exactly that same boundary:

```text
J_P,ij = J_P(B_ij)
```

This prevents another construction-recovery loop.

Forbidden:

```text
compute curvature over one boundary
compute source current over another boundary
compare them anyway
```

---

## 3. Allowed ledger events

`J_P` may use only provenance/source/order events such as:

```text
source_injection
source_removal
branch_birth
branch_prune
dependency_addition
dependency_break
repair_event
closure_event
provenance_relabel
order_boundary_crossing
```

Each event must carry:

```text
event_id
ordered_index
event_type
node_id
source_label
magnitude
dependencies
prior_event_ids
valid_order_arrow
boundary_id
```

Forbidden inputs:

```text
R_Γ
D_Γ R_Γ
E_Γ
weak_target
scalar_holonomy
posthoc_fit_residual
```

---

## 4. Component currents

Define:

```text
J_P,ij
=
J_source,ij
+
J_dependency,ij
+
J_repair,ij
+
J_order,ij
+
J_label,ij
```

### Source current

```text
J_source,ij
=
signed_sum(source_injection − source_removal across B_ij)
```

### Dependency current

```text
J_dependency,ij
=
signed_sum(dependency_addition − dependency_break across B_ij)
```

### Repair current

```text
J_repair,ij
=
signed_sum(repair_event − unresolved_break across B_ij)
```

### Order current

```text
J_order,ij
=
signed_sum(order_boundary_crossing)
−
order_arrow_violations
```

### Label current

```text
J_label,ij
=
signed_sum(provenance_relabel with conserved lineage)
−
lineage_breaks
```

Each component must be reported separately.

A small aggregate `J_P` cannot hide a failed component or order violation.

---

## 5. Representation problem

`D_Γ R_Γ` is endomorphism-valued:

```text
(D_Γ R_Γ)_{ij} ∈ End_alg(G_i)
```

So `J_P` must be represented in the same comparison space.

Define a representation map:

```text
ρ_P : provenance/source current → End_alg(G_i)
```

Then the identity is really:

```text
D_Γ R_Γ = ρ_P(J_P)
```

or, by notation convention:

```text
D_Γ R_Γ = J_P
```

where `J_P` already means the represented current.

Critical rule:

```text
ρ_P may use ledger event structure only.
ρ_P may not use R_Γ, D_Γ R_Γ, E_Γ, weak_target, or fitted residuals.
```

---

## 6. Current admissibility

Define:

```text
J_boundary_pass
```

all events used in `J_P` share the boundary of the comparison.

```text
J_order_pass
```

retained-order arrows are valid, or violations are explicitly counted.

```text
J_independence_pass
```

forbidden fields are absent.

```text
J_conservation_pass
```

event accounting balances internally before curvature comparison.

Then:

```text
J_P_admissible
=
J_boundary_pass
∧
J_order_pass
∧
J_independence_pass
∧
J_conservation_pass
```

If `J_P_admissible` fails, the identity cannot be promoted.

---

## 7. Output schema

### `provenance_events.csv`

```text
event_id
ordered_index
event_type
node_id
source_label
magnitude
dependencies
prior_event_ids
valid_order_arrow
boundary_id
```

### `source_currents.csv`

```text
comparison_id
boundary_id
J_source
J_dependency
J_repair
J_order
J_label
J_P_serialized
rho_P_map_id
J_P_norm
J_boundary_pass
J_order_pass
J_independence_pass
J_conservation_pass
J_P_admissible
```

### `current_manifest.json`

```text
forbidden_fields_absent
rho_P_definition
event_accounting_rule
boundary_alignment_rule
claim_boundary
```

---

## 8. Killer current nulls

### `N_source_shuffle`

Preserve event counts and magnitudes but shuffle boundary assignments.

Expected:

```text
same source statistics
wrong boundary current
E_Γ fails
```

### `N_order_reverse`

Preserve events but reverse retained-order dependencies.

Expected:

```text
same event counts
J_order fails
E_Γ fails or J_P inadmissible
```

### `N_label_shuffle`

Preserve provenance-label counts but break lineage continuity.

Expected:

```text
same labels
broken provenance current
E_Γ fails
```

### `N_current_zero`

Force:

```text
J_P = 0
```

in active-source regimes.

Expected:

```text
source-balanced identity passes
source-free identity fails
```

---

## 9. Frozen nonclaims

```text
J_P is not a target fitted after seeing curvature.
J_P is not weak_target.
J_P is not scalar holonomy.
J_P cannot be computed from E_Γ.
Source-free closure cannot be tested before source-balanced closure.
```

---

## Final statement

V1689.3 freezes `J_P` as a ledger-derived provenance/source/pruning-order current over the same comparison boundary as `D_Γ R_Γ`.

The identity is:

```text
D_Γ R_Γ = J_P
```

but only after:

```text
Γ_R admissibility passes
R_Γ admissibility passes
comparison admissibility passes
J_P admissibility passes
```

This prevents the right-hand side from becoming another constructed target.

END EXACT DOCUMENT

UTF8-BYTES 863

# V1689.9 — Deriving `ρ_P` from Provenance Event Action on `G_p`

**Verdict:** `RHO_P_EVENT_ACTION_DERIVATION_FROZEN_NO_EXECUTION`

## Why this step is necessary

V1689.8 showed that arbitrary maps:

```text
ρ_P : provenance/source current → End_alg(G_i)
```

leave high residual:

```text
E_Γ = D_ΓR_Γ − ρ_P(J_P)
```

and introduce scale mismatch.

So `ρ_P` cannot be chosen as a convenient embedding.

It must be derived from the way provenance/source events act on the generated retained algebra.

---

## 1. Local algebra

At a retained sector:

```text
G_p = ⟨B_p, O3_p, H4⊥_p, S_p, Π_p, ⊕_p⟩
```

A provenance event:

```text
e ∈ Π_p
```

acts on the local algebra:

```text
A_e : G_p → G'_p
```

The representation map is the linearized endomorphism induced by this event action:

```text
ρ_e = dA_e|_{G_p}
```

The boundary current representation is:

```text
ρ_P(J_P,ij)
=
Σ_{e ∈ Events(B_ij)}
σ(e) · magnitude(e) · ρ_e
```

where:

```text
σ(e)
```

is the retained-order/source orientation of the event crossing the boundary.

---

## 2. Event actions

### Source injection/removal

A source event modifies the source-supported branch/source subspace.

```text
ρ_source(e) = ± P_S(e)
```

where `P_S(e)` is a projector onto the source-affected subspace.

Positive for injection, negative for removal.

---

### Branch birth/prune

A branch birth adds a retained generator:

```text
ρ_birth(e) = + P_B(k)
```

A branch prune suppresses/removes one:

```text
ρ_prune(e) = − P_B(k)
```

where:

```text
P_B(k) = b_k b_k^T / (||b_k||^2 + ε)
```

---

### Dependency addition/break

A dependency event acts on a branch pair or provenance-supported incidence relation.

For branches `b_a`, `b_b`:

```text
I_dep(a,b)
=
(b_a − b_b)(b_a − b_b)^T
/
(||b_a − b_b||^2 + ε)
```

Then:

```text
ρ_dep_add(e)   = + I_dep(a,b)
ρ_dep_break(e) = − I_dep(a,b)
```

---

### Repair / closure event

A repair event acts on the defect subspace it repairs.

Allowed subspaces:

```text
product defect subspace
O3 associator defect subspace
H4⊥ residual defect subspace
```

Examples:

```text
ρ_repair_product(e) = + P_product_defect(e)
ρ_repair_O3(e)      = + P_O3
ρ_repair_H4(e)      = + P_H4
```

where:

```text
P_O3 = O3 O3^T / (||O3||^2 + ε)
P_H4 = H4⊥ H4⊥^T / (||H4⊥||^2 + ε)
```

Critical rule:

```text
These projectors must be constructed before E_Γ is computed.
```

They may not be fitted from the residual.

---

### Order-boundary crossing

An order event carries retained-order orientation.

```text
ρ_order(e)
=
σ_order(e) · I_order(e)
```

where `σ_order(e)` is determined by the pruning-order direction.

If the event reverses retained order, it contributes a violation rather than a valid current.

---

### Provenance relabel

A relabel event acts as a provenance-label permutation/conjugation on the labeled subspace.

```text
ρ_label(e) = + P_label
```

if lineage is conserved.

If lineage breaks:

```text
ρ_label(e) = − P_label
```

or the current becomes inadmissible, depending on the rule.

---

## 3. Full current representation

The represented source/provenance current is:

```text
ρ_P(J_P,ij)
=
Σ_e σ(e) magnitude(e) ρ_e
```

Component form:

```text
ρ_P(J_P)
=
ρ_source(J_source)
+
ρ_dependency(J_dependency)
+
ρ_repair(J_repair)
+
ρ_order(J_order)
+
ρ_label(J_label)
```

This object lives in:

```text
End_alg(G_i)
```

so it can be compared to:

```text
D_Γ R_Γ
```

---

## 4. Normalization

`ρ_P` must be normalized by the boundary algebra scale:

```text
||G_i||_alg
=
||B_i|| + ||O3_i|| + ||H4⊥_i|| + ||S_i|| + ε
```

not by:

```text
E_Γ
D_ΓR_Γ
fitted target performance
```

The normalization must be known before the identity residual is computed.

---

## 5. Forbidden dependencies

`ρ_P` may not use:

```text
R_Γ
D_ΓR_Γ
E_Γ
weak_target
scalar holonomy
fitted coefficients
post-hoc residuals
```

This is the firewall against construction recovery.

---

## 6. New required table: `event_actions.csv`

Future execution must emit:

```text
event_id
comparison_id
boundary_id
node_id
event_type
event_orientation
event_magnitude
affected_subspace_id
rho_e_serialized
rho_e_norm
rho_e_source
rho_uses_forbidden_fields
```

Then `source_currents.csv` must aggregate:

```text
ρ_P(J_P,ij)
=
Σ_e σ(e) magnitude(e) ρ_e
```

---

## 7. Killer nulls for `ρ_P`

### `N_event_action_shuffle`

Preserve event counts and magnitudes but assign events to wrong affected algebra subspaces.

Expected:

```text
same event statistics
wrong ρ_P
E_Γ fails
```

### `N_order_reverse`

Reverse event orientation.

Expected:

```text
same event counts
opposite source/order current
E_Γ fails or current inadmissible
```

### `N_projector_shuffle`

Preserve projector norms but shuffle branch/O3/H4 target subspaces.

Expected:

```text
same ρ_e norms
wrong algebra action
E_Γ fails
```

### `N_current_zero`

Set:

```text
ρ_P(J_P) = 0
```

in active-source regimes.

Expected:

```text
source-free closure fails when source-balanced closure is needed
```

---

## 8. Final identity

The law candidate is:

```text
D_Γ R_Γ = ρ_P(J_P)
```

with residual:

```text
E_Γ = D_Γ R_Γ − ρ_P(J_P)
```

But this identity cannot be tested until:

```text
Γ_R is admissible
R_Γ is admissible
D_ΓR_Γ is admissible
ρ_P is emitted from event actions
null pipelines regenerate event-action maps upstream
```

---

## Final statement

`ρ_P` is not a tunable bridge.

It is the event-action representation of provenance/source/pruning-order current on the generated retained algebra.

This is the required bridge from ledger current to curvature compatibility.

EXACT DOCUMENT ResearchHistory/UQCF-GEM/research/physical-bridge/groupoid_identity_ownership/v1689_recovery/inspect_archives.py
UTF8-BYTES 3565
"""Read original V1689 ZIPs; no historical scientific module is executed.

Run from the packet directory: python3 inspect_archives.py
Archives are inputs; extracted/ and INSPECTION.json are deterministic outputs.
This verifies archive bytes/schema facts, not the archived scientific claims.
"""
from pathlib import Path
import csv
import hashlib
import io
import json
import zipfile

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent

def identities(data):
    return {
        "bytes": len(data),
        "sha256": hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest(),
        "git_blob": hashlib.sha1(b"blob " + str(len(data)).encode() + b"\0" + data).hexdigest(),
    }

def inspect():
    archives = []
    expected = json.loads((ROOT / "SOURCE_PROVENANCE.json").read_text())
    expected_names = {x["archive"] for x in expected["archives"]}
    actual_names = {p.name for p in (ROOT / "archives").glob("*.zip")}
    if actual_names != expected_names:
        raise ValueError("archive identity set differs")
    for source in sorted(expected["archives"], key=lambda x: x["archive"]):
        path = ROOT / "archives" / source["archive"]
        data = path.read_bytes()
        if identities(data) != source["downloaded_bytes"]:
            raise ValueError("archive hash/length mismatch: " + path.name)
        members = []
        with zipfile.ZipFile(io.BytesIO(data)) as z:
            if z.testzip() is not None:
                raise ValueError("CRC failure: " + path.name)
            names = z.namelist()
            if len(names) != len(set(names)):
                raise ValueError("duplicate ZIP member")
            for info in z.infolist():
                if info.is_dir():
                    continue
                payload = z.read(info)
                out = ROOT / "extracted" / path.stem / info.filename
                if not out.resolve().is_relative_to((ROOT / "extracted" / path.stem).resolve()):
                    raise ValueError("unsafe ZIP path")
                out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
                out.write_bytes(payload)
                item = {"path": info.filename, **identities(payload), "crc32": f"{info.CRC:08x}"}
                if info.filename.endswith(".csv"):
                    reader = csv.DictReader(io.StringIO(payload.decode("utf-8")))
                    rows = list(reader)
                    item.update(headers=reader.fieldnames, row_count=len(rows))
                    if "event_type" in (reader.fieldnames or []):
                        types = sorted({row["event_type"] for row in rows})
                        item.update(event_types=types, branch_prune_count=sum(row["event_type"] == "branch_prune" for row in rows))
                members.append(item)
        archives.append({"archive": path.name, **identities(data), "members": members})
    result = {
        "schema": "uqcf-v1689-original-archive-inspection-v1",
        "scope": "Original ZIP/member integrity and literal table schema only; no scientific reproduction or acceptance.",
        "archives": archives,
        "source_code_members": [a["archive"] + ":" + m["path"] for a in archives for m in a["members"] if Path(m["path"]).suffix.lower() in {".py", ".ipynb", ".jl", ".r", ".m", ".js", ".ts", ".cpp", ".c", ".sh"}],
    }
    (ROOT / "INSPECTION.json").write_text(json.dumps(result, indent=2) + "\n")
    print(json.dumps({"archives": len(archives), "members": sum(len(a["members"]) for a in archives), "source_code_members": result["source_code_members"], "zip_and_member_crc": "PASS"}))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    inspect()

END EXACT DOCUMENT
